```sql
       AND (os_ablation_max_um IS NULL
            OR (os_ablation_max_um > 0 AND os_ablation_max_um <= 200)));

-- 差异字段数不得为负
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_version ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_version_diff
    CHECK (diff_field_count IS NULL OR diff_field_count >= 0);
```

**COMMENT：**

```sql
COMMENT ON TABLE m11_plan_version
    IS '方案版本表：每次提交审核生成一个完整不可变版本快照，支撑方案对比与追溯';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_version.snapshot_json IS '本版完整方案快照 JSONB（方案+术前+切削+晶体+风险全量）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_version.snapshot_hash IS '快照 SHA-256，防篡改校验';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_version.diff_summary IS '与上一版差异摘要 JSONB（字段→旧值→新值）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_version.is_current IS '是否为当前生效版本，每方案至多一条';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **完整快照而非增量差异**：若用差异链，重建第 5 版需回放 1~4 版。任一环节损坏即导致后续全部不可读。医疗记录的可用性优先于存储成本，故每版存完整快照（单版约 8~15KB，年增 24,000 版约 300MB，可接受）。
2. **`diff_summary` 与 `snapshot_json` 并存**：P07 对比页需展示「改了什么」，若每次都加载两个完整快照做 diff 则响应慢。`diff_summary` 预计算好差异，页面直接读；需要完整细节时才加载 `snapshot_json`。
3. **关键参数摘要独立列**：`od_oz_mm`/`od_rsb_um` 等 9 个高频对比字段单独成列，使「对比两版 OZ 变化」这类查询无需解析 JSONB，可直接索引与聚合。
4. **`uk_m11_plan_version_current`**：保证每方案恰好一个当前版本。这使「取当前方案参数」的查询退化为唯一索引查找。

**分区策略：** 无（24,000/年）。

---

### 4.10 表：`m11_plan_risk`（方案风险提示表）

**用途：** 记录每个方案命中的**全部风险提示项**（一行 = 一个风险项 × 眼别）。风险项由规则引擎计算得出，覆盖任务要求的五类：RSB 红线、角膜厚度不足、瞳孔过大、高阶像差、干眼，另含禁忌类与术中类风险。

**写入方：** 本子模块（P04/P05 计算后、P08 风险提示页生成）。**只读方：** 本子模块（P08 展示）、审核医师（审核时必看）、M11-05（知情同意引用）。

**关键设计：**

1. **`risk_code` 枚举固定 20 项**（附录 B.4），每项有固定阈值、等级与处置建议。用编码而非自由文本，使风险可统计、可聚合、可追溯。
2. **`is_blocking` 区分阻断级与提示级**：`FORBIDDEN` 级风险（如 RSB < 250μm、圆锥角膜可疑）直接阻断方案审核；`HIGH` 级需科主任终审；`MEDIUM`/`LOW` 仅提示。
3. **`acknowledged` 记录医师已阅确认**：知情同意的有效性要求医师已向患者说明风险。医师必须逐项确认，未确认的高风险项阻断审核提交。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| risk_no | VARCHAR(40) | Y | NULL | 风险记录号 `RSK` + YYYYMMDD + 6 位 |
| plan_id | BIGINT | N | — | 方案 ID（`m11_plan.id`） |
| plan_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 方案号（冗余） |
| patient_id | BIGINT | N | — | 患者 ID（只读引用 M03） |
| eye_side | VARCHAR(8) | N | 'NA' | 风险涉及眼别 `OD`/`OS`/`OU`/`NA` |
| **风险项组** | | | | |
| risk_code | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 风险编码，见附录 B.4（20 项） |
| risk_name | VARCHAR(128) | N | — | 风险中文名称（快照） |
| risk_category | VARCHAR(24) | N | — | 风险类别 `ABLATION_SAFETY`/`CORNEAL`/`OCULAR_SURFACE`/`OPTICAL_QUALITY`/`INTRAOCULAR`/`CONTRAINDICATION`/`PROCEDURAL` |
| risk_level | VARCHAR(16) | N | — | 风险等级 `LOW`/`MEDIUM`/`HIGH`/`FORBIDDEN` |
| is_blocking | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否阻断方案（`FORBIDDEN` 为 TRUE） |
| **触发数据组** | | | | |
| trigger_value | NUMERIC(10,2) | Y | NULL | 触发值（如 RSB 240.00、瞳孔 7.50） |
| trigger_value_str | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 触发值文本形式（非数值型风险用，如 `KERATOCONUS_SUSPECT`） |
| threshold_value | NUMERIC(10,2) | Y | NULL | 阈值（如 250.00） |
| threshold_op | VARCHAR(8) | Y | NULL | 比较运算符 `LT`/`LTE`/`GT`/`GTE`/`EQ`/`NE` |
| trigger_unit | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 单位 `μm`/`mm`/`D`/`%`/`NA` |
| rule_code | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 触发规则编码 |
| rule_version | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 规则版本 |
| **说明与处置组** | | | | |
| risk_desc | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 风险描述（面向医师的完整说明） |
| patient_desc | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 患者版说明（通俗表述，供知情同意引用） |
| suggestion | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 处置建议（如「改做 ICL」「缩小光学区至 6.00mm」） |
| alternative_hint | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 替代术式提示 |
| **确认组** | | | | |
| acknowledged | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 医师是否已阅确认 |
| acknowledged_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 确认医师 ID |
| acknowledged_by_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 确认医师姓名（快照） |
| acknowledged_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 确认时间 |
| ack_remark | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 确认备注 |
| patient_notified | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否已告知患者 |
| patient_notified_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 告知患者时间 |
| **状态组** | | | | |
| is_active | BOOLEAN | N | TRUE | 是否仍然有效（参数变更后旧风险失效） |
| resolved | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否已消除（如已缩小 OZ 使 RSB 达标） |
| resolved_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 消除时间 |
| resolved_by_change | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 消除原因（做了哪些调整） |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引：**

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_plan
    ON m11_plan_risk (plan_id, risk_level, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_plan_risk_item
    ON m11_plan_risk (plan_id, eye_side, risk_code)
    WHERE is_active = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_blocking
    ON m11_plan_risk (plan_id) WHERE is_blocking = TRUE AND is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_unack
    ON m11_plan_risk (plan_id) WHERE is_blocking = TRUE AND acknowledged = FALSE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_patient
    ON m11_plan_risk (patient_id, risk_code, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_code
    ON m11_plan_risk (risk_code, created_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_level
    ON m11_plan_risk (risk_level, created_at DESC) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_risk_no
    ON m11_plan_risk (risk_no) WHERE risk_no IS NOT NULL;
```

**约束：**

```sql
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_eye
    CHECK (eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS', 'OU', 'NA'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_level
    CHECK (risk_level IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'FORBIDDEN'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_category
    CHECK (risk_category IN ('ABLATION_SAFETY', 'CORNEAL', 'OCULAR_SURFACE',
                             'OPTICAL_QUALITY', 'INTRAOCULAR',
                             'CONTRAINDICATION', 'PROCEDURAL'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_op
    CHECK (threshold_op IS NULL
        OR threshold_op IN ('LT', 'LTE', 'GT', 'GTE', 'EQ', 'NE'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_unit
    CHECK (trigger_unit IS NULL
        OR trigger_unit IN ('μm', 'mm', 'D', '%', 'NA'));

-- ★ FORBIDDEN 级必须阻断
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_blocking
    CHECK (risk_level <> 'FORBIDDEN' OR is_blocking = TRUE);

-- 阻断级必须已告知患者才能提交审核
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_notify
    CHECK (is_blocking = FALSE OR resolved = TRUE
        OR patient_notified = TRUE);

-- 确认必须有确认人与时间
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_ack
    CHECK (acknowledged = FALSE
        OR (acknowledged_by IS NOT NULL AND acknowledged_at IS NOT NULL));

-- 告知必须有告知时间
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_notify_pair
    CHECK (patient_notified = FALSE OR patient_notified_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 已消除必须有消除时间
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_resolved
    CHECK (resolved = FALSE OR resolved_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 已消除的风险不得仍为阻断状态
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_resolved_blocking
    CHECK (resolved = FALSE OR is_blocking = FALSE OR is_active = FALSE);

-- 触发值与阈值至少有一项（文本型风险用 trigger_value_str）
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_trigger
    CHECK (trigger_value IS NOT NULL OR trigger_value_str IS NOT NULL);

-- 数值型风险必须有阈值与运算符
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_risk ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_risk_threshold
    CHECK (trigger_value IS NULL
        OR (threshold_value IS NOT NULL AND threshold_op IS NOT NULL));
```

**COMMENT：**

```sql
COMMENT ON TABLE m11_plan_risk
    IS '方案风险提示表：每个方案命中的风险项，含阻断级与提示级，供审核与知情同意引用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_risk.risk_code IS '风险编码，共 20 项，见附录 B.4';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_risk.is_blocking IS '是否阻断方案，FORBIDDEN 级恒为 TRUE';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_risk.trigger_value IS '触发值（如 RSB 240.00μm、暗瞳 7.50mm）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_risk.patient_desc IS '患者版风险说明（通俗表述），知情同意引用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_risk.acknowledged IS '医师是否已逐项阅确认，未确认的阻断级风险不得提交审核';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **编码化而非自由文本**：风险若写成自由文本，无法统计「最常见的风险是什么」「某术式的风险分布」。20 个固定编码使风险可聚合、可比较、可训练模型。
2. **`is_blocking` 与 `patient_notified` 联动约束**：阻断级风险必须已告知患者才能提交审核（`ck_m11_plan_risk_notify`）。这是知情同意制度的数字化落地——不是签个字就行，而是每个具体的严重风险都逐项告知过。
3. **`is_active` 与 `resolved` 双标记**：参数调整后旧风险需失效（`is_active = FALSE`），若通过调整参数消除了风险则记 `resolved = TRUE` 并写明做了什么调整。二者区别在于「参数变了所以这条不再适用」与「主动干预后风险确实消除」。
4. **`patient_desc` 独立列**：医师版描述（`risk_desc`）充满专业术语，不能直接给患者看。患者版说明需用通俗语言（如「术后夜间看灯可能有光圈」而非「高阶像差引起眩光」）。独立字段保证知情同意的可读性。

**分区策略：** 无（60,000/年）。

---

### 4.11 表：`m11_plan_consent_snap`（知情同意方案快照表）

**用途：** 记录**知情同意签署时的方案完整快照**，与方案 1:1。这是本子模块的**法律证据表**：一旦发生医疗纠纷，需证明「患者签署同意书时，方案就是这个内容」，而这与签署后的方案修订无关。

**写入方：** 本子模块（P06 签署时由 M11-05 触发或本模块直接生成）。**只读方：** 医政科、法务、纠纷处理。

**关键设计：**

1. **`snapshot_json` + `snapshot_hash` + `pdf_file_id` 三重固化**：JSON 供系统读取，hash 供完整性校验，PDF 供法务出示。三者绑定签署时间与签署人，构成完整证据链。
2. **签署后永不修改**：`is_sealed` 标记一旦为 TRUE，任何字段都不可更新（应用层强制 + CHECK 兜底）。
3. **`risk_codes_snapshot` 存当时告知的风险清单**：证明「签署时告知了哪些风险」，而非事后补充。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| consent_no | VARCHAR(40) | Y | NULL | 同意书编号 `CNS` + YYYYMMDD + 4 位 |
| plan_id | BIGINT | N | — | 方案 ID（`m11_plan.id`，1:1） |
| plan_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 方案号（冗余） |
| patient_id | BIGINT | N | — | 患者 ID（只读引用 M03） |
| plan_version | INT | N | 1 | 签署时的方案版本号 |
| **签署内容组** | | | | |
| consent_type | VARCHAR(24) | N | 'SURGERY' | 同意书类型 `SURGERY`（手术）/`ANESTHESIA`（麻醉）/`IOL_IMPLANT`（晶体植入）/`RE_TREATMENT`（再手术） |
| consent_template_code | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 同意书模板编码（M01 文档模板） |
| consent_template_version | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 模板版本 |
| snapshot_json | JSONB | N | — | **签署时方案完整快照**（术式+切削/晶体+风险清单） |
| snapshot_hash | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 快照 SHA-256 |
| risk_codes_snapshot | JSONB | N | — | 签署时已告知的风险编码数组 |
| risk_count | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 已告知风险条数 |
| **签署人与时间组** | | | | |
| signer_name | VARCHAR(64) | N | — | 签署人姓名（患者本人或法定监护人） |
| signer_relation | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'SELF' | 签署人与患者关系 `SELF`/`FATHER`/`MOTHER`/`GUARDIAN`/`OTHER` |
| signer_id_card | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 签署人身份证号（加密存储） |
| signed_at | TIMESTAMP | N | — | 签署时间 |
| witness_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 见证人姓名（未成年或特殊情形） |
| witness_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 见证人职工 ID |
| **医师告知组** | | | | |
| inform_doctor_id | BIGINT | N | — | 告知医师 ID（须为签署方案的主刀或设计医师） |
| inform_doctor_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 告知医师姓名（快照） |
| informed_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 告知完成时间 |
| inform_duration_minutes | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 告知时长（分钟） |
| qa_record | JSONB | Y | NULL | 患者提问与答复记录数组 |
| **文件组** | | | | |
| pdf_file_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 同意书 PDF 文件 ID（文件服务） |
| pdf_url | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | PDF 访问地址（签名 URL） |
| signature_image_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 电子签名图片 ID |
| scan_file_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 纸质扫描件 ID（可选） |
| **状态组** | | | | |
| is_sealed | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否已固化（签署完成后 TRUE，此后不可改） |
| sealed_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 固化时间 |
| is_revoked | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否已撤销（患者反悔） |
| revoked_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 撤销时间 |
| revoke_reason | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 撤销原因（≥10 字） |
| revoke_witness | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 撤销见证人 |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| created_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 创建人 |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引：**

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_plan_consent_snap_plan
    ON m11_plan_consent_snap (plan_id) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m11_plan_consent_snap_no
    ON m11_plan_consent_snap (consent_no) WHERE consent_no IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_consent_snap_patient
    ON m11_plan_consent_snap (patient_id, signed_at DESC) WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_consent_snap_hash
    ON m11_plan_consent_snap (snapshot_hash) WHERE snapshot_hash IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_consent_snap_revoked
    ON m11_plan_consent_snap (revoked_at) WHERE is_revoked = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_consent_snap_unsealed
    ON m11_plan_consent_snap (signed_at) WHERE is_sealed = FALSE AND is_deleted = FALSE;
```

**约束：**

```sql
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_type
    CHECK (consent_type IN ('SURGERY', 'ANESTHESIA', 'IOL_IMPLANT', 'RE_TREATMENT'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_relation
    CHECK (signer_relation IN ('SELF', 'FATHER', 'MOTHER', 'GUARDIAN', 'OTHER'));

-- 快照必须是 JSON 对象
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_snapshot
    CHECK (jsonb_typeof(snapshot_json) = 'object');

-- 风险清单必须是数组
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_risks
    CHECK (jsonb_typeof(risk_codes_snapshot) = 'array');

-- 已告知风险数不得为负
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_risk_count
    CHECK (risk_count IS NULL OR risk_count >= 0);

-- ★ 非本人签署必须有见证人或关系说明
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_signer
    CHECK (signer_relation = 'SELF'
        OR (witness_name IS NOT NULL OR signer_id_card IS NOT NULL));

-- 告知时长合理区间
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_duration
    CHECK (inform_duration_minutes IS NULL
        OR inform_duration_minutes BETWEEN 1 AND 240);

-- 固化必须有固化时间
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_sealed
    CHECK (is_sealed = FALSE OR sealed_at IS NOT NULL);

-- ★ 固化后必须有 PDF 与 hash
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_sealed_files
    CHECK (is_sealed = FALSE
        OR (pdf_file_id IS NOT NULL AND snapshot_hash IS NOT NULL));

-- 撤销必须有时间与原因（≥10 字）
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_revoked
    CHECK (is_revoked = FALSE
        OR (revoked_at IS NOT NULL
            AND revoke_reason IS NOT NULL
            AND char_length(btrim(revoke_reason)) >= 10));

-- 撤销必须在固化之后
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_revoke_order
    CHECK (is_revoked = FALSE OR (is_sealed = TRUE AND sealed_at <= revoked_at));

-- 签署时间不得早于告知完成
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_order
    CHECK (informed_at IS NULL OR signed_at >= informed_at);

-- 方案版本号范围
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_version
    CHECK (plan_version BETWEEN 1 AND 999);

-- ★ 已固化记录不可逻辑删除（证据保全）
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_consent_immutable
    CHECK (is_sealed = FALSE OR is_deleted = FALSE);
```

**COMMENT：**

```sql
COMMENT ON TABLE m11_plan_consent_snap
    IS '知情同意方案快照表：签署时点的方案完整冻结，法律证据表，1:1 且固化后不可变';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_consent_snap.snapshot_json IS '签署时方案完整快照（术式+切削/晶体+风险清单）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_consent_snap.snapshot_hash IS '快照 SHA-256，防篡改，与 PDF 绑定构成证据链';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_consent_snap.risk_codes_snapshot IS '签署时已告知的风险编码数组，证明告知范围';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_consent_snap.is_sealed IS '是否已固化，固化后任何字段不可更新且不可删除';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_consent_snap.qa_record IS '患者提问与医师答复记录数组，证明充分告知';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **快照与方案分离的理由**：方案在签署后仍可能因患者反悔、医师判断变更而修订（并生成新版本）。但「患者签的是哪个方案」这一事实必须永久固定。若直接引用 `m11_plan_version`，则版本表被清理或损坏时证据链断裂。
2. **`is_sealed` + `ck_m11_plan_consent_immutable`**：固化后连逻辑删除都不允许。医疗纠纷可能发生在术后数年，证据必须比业务数据的生命周期更长。
3. **`qa_record` 记录问答**：知情同意的有效性不只靠签字，还要求「充分告知」。记录患者问了什么、医师怎么答，是证明充分告知的直接证据。这在纠纷中是决定性的。
4. **`risk_codes_snapshot` 独立于 `snapshot_json`**：风险清单是最常被单独查询的证据（「是否告知过术后光圈风险」）。独立成列可建 GIN 索引，直接按风险编码检索。

**分区策略：** 无（8,000/年）。

---

### 4.12 表：`m11_plan_log`（方案操作日志表）

**用途：** 记录手术方案全流程的**每一个写操作**，用于合规审计、纠纷追溯、异常排查。屈光手术涉及不可逆的组织切削与眼内植入，全过程可追溯是监管硬要求。

**写入方：** 本子模块所有 Service 层的写操作方法（统一由 `PlanLogService.record()` 写入）。**只读方：** 质控科、医政科、M02-06 审计中心。

**关键设计：** 按 `created_at` 月度 RANGE 分区，**必须有 DEFAULT 分区兜底**（共享规范 §2.6 硬要求），保留 24 个月。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键（分区表复合主键之一） |
| log_no | VARCHAR(48) | Y | NULL | 日志流水号 `PLG` + yyyyMMddHHmmss + 6 位 |
| plan_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 方案 ID |
| plan_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 方案号（冗余） |
| patient_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 患者 ID（只读引用 M03） |
| entity_type | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 操作对象 `PLAN`/`PREOP_SNAP`/`ABLATION`/`ABLATION_CALC`/`IOL_CALC`/`AUDIT`/`VERSION`/`RISK`/`CONSENT` |
| entity_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 操作对象 ID |
| action | VARCHAR(40) | N | — | 操作动作，见附录 B.6（28 个） |
| action_desc | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 动作中文描述 |
| from_status | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 变更前状态 |
| to_status | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 变更后状态 |
| changed_fields | JSONB | Y | NULL | 变更字段明细（字段 → 旧值 → 新值） |
| snapshot | JSONB | Y | NULL | 关键快照（重大操作存完整对象） |
| operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 操作人 ID |
| operator_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 操作人姓名（快照） |
| operator_role | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 操作人角色 `DOCTOR`/`CHIEF_DOCTOR`/`QC`/`NURSE`/`SYSTEM` |
| dept_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 科室 ID |
| client_ip | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 客户端 IP |
| user_agent | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 客户端 UA |
| trace_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 链路追踪 ID（与响应 trace_id 一致） |
| result | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'SUCCESS' | 执行结果 `SUCCESS`/`FAIL` |
| error_code | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 失败时的错误码 |
| error_message | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 失败原因 |
| duration_ms | INT | Y | NULL | 操作耗时（毫秒） |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 日志时间（分区键） |

**索引：**

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_plan
    ON m11_plan_log (plan_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_patient
    ON m11_plan_log (patient_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_entity
    ON m11_plan_log (entity_type, entity_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_action
    ON m11_plan_log (action, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_operator
    ON m11_plan_log (operator_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_fail
    ON m11_plan_log (created_at DESC) WHERE result = 'FAIL';

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_trace
    ON m11_plan_log (trace_id) WHERE trace_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_no
    ON m11_plan_log (log_no) WHERE log_no IS NOT NULL;
```

**约束：**

```sql
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_no_format
    CHECK (log_no IS NULL OR log_no ~ '^PLG[0-9]{20}$');

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_entity
    CHECK (entity_type IN ('PLAN', 'PREOP_SNAP', 'ABLATION', 'ABLATION_CALC',
                           'IOL_CALC', 'AUDIT', 'VERSION', 'RISK', 'CONSENT'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_result
    CHECK (result IN ('SUCCESS', 'FAIL'));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_role
    CHECK (operator_role IS NULL
        OR operator_role IN ('DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_DOCTOR', 'QC', 'NURSE', 'SYSTEM'));

-- 失败必须填错误码与信息
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_fail
    CHECK (result = 'SUCCESS'
        OR (error_code IS NOT NULL AND error_message IS NOT NULL));

-- 成功不得残留错误信息
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_success
    CHECK (result = 'FAIL' OR (error_code IS NULL AND error_message IS NULL));

ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_duration
    CHECK (duration_ms IS NULL OR duration_ms >= 0);

-- 除风险表外，其余实体必须能关联到方案
ALTER TABLE m11_plan_log ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m11_plan_log_plan_ref
    CHECK (entity_type IN ('PLAN', 'RISK') OR plan_id IS NOT NULL);
```

**分区策略：**

```sql
CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log (
    id          BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
    log_no      VARCHAR(48),
    plan_id     BIGINT,
    plan_no     VARCHAR(32),
    patient_id  BIGINT,
    entity_type VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    entity_id   BIGINT,
    action      VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    action_desc VARCHAR(256),
    from_status VARCHAR(24),
    to_status   VARCHAR(24),
    changed_fields JSONB,
    snapshot    JSONB,
    operator_id BIGINT,
    operator_name VARCHAR(64),
    operator_role VARCHAR(32),
    dept_id     BIGINT,
    client_ip   VARCHAR(64),
    user_agent  VARCHAR(256),
    trace_id    VARCHAR(64),
    result      VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SUCCESS',
    error_code  VARCHAR(16),
    error_message VARCHAR(512),
    duration_ms INT,
    created_at  TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);

COMMENT ON TABLE m11_plan_log IS '方案操作日志表：按月 RANGE 分区 + DEFAULT 分区兜底，保留 24 个月';

-- 预建分区示例（2026-10 至 2027-01）
CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202610 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01') TO ('2026-11-01');
CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202611 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01');
CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202612 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-12-01') TO ('2027-01-01');
CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202701 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2027-01-01') TO ('2027-02-01');

-- ★ DEFAULT 分区兜底：防止未预建月份写入失败（共享规范硬要求）
CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_pdefault PARTITION OF m11_plan_log DEFAULT;

-- 分区自动维护：APScheduler 每月 20 日预建下下月分区
-- 保留策略：DETACH 24 个月前的分区并归档冷存储
```

**COMMENT：**

```sql
COMMENT ON TABLE m11_plan_log
    IS '方案操作日志表：28 个动作全留痕，按月 RANGE 分区，DEFAULT 分区兜底，保留 24 个月';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_log.action IS '操作动作，28 个取值，见附录 B.6';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_log.changed_fields IS '变更字段明细 JSONB（字段→旧值→新值）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_log.snapshot IS '重大操作完整对象快照';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m11_plan_log.trace_id IS '链路追踪 ID，与接口响应 trace_id 一致';
```

**设计说明：**

1. **DEFAULT 分区是硬要求**：若无 DEFAULT 分区，一旦某月的分区未被预建，写入会直接报错导致业务失败。共享规范 §2.6 明确要求必须有 DEFAULT 分区兜底。
2. **`result='FAIL'` 也记录**：失败的授权校验、失败的 RSB 校验、被驳回的提交都是重要信号——失败率突增说明权限配置或规则有问题。部分索引 `idx_m11_plan_log_fail` 只索引失败行，排查时精确高效。
3. **`changed_fields` 与 `snapshot` 分工**：常规字段修改记增量（体积小）；重大操作（审核通过、方案固化、切削参数确认、晶体度数确认）记完整快照（可完整还原当时上下文）。
4. **`entity_type` 覆盖 9 类实体**：日志不仅记方案主表，还记术前快照、切削、晶体、审核、版本、风险、同意的操作。这样任一环节出问题都能独立追溯。

**分区策略：** 按月 `RANGE (created_at)`，保留 24 个月，DEFAULT 分区兜底。

---
### 4.13 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义

```python
# app/models/m11/plan.py
"""M11-04 手术方案与切削计算 —— SQLAlchemy 2.0 ORM 模型"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity,
    Index, Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, Text, func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.models.base import Base


class M11Plan(Base):
    """手术方案主表。一患一次屈光手术方案一条。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 身份组
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    patient_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    gender: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8))
    age: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    id_card_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    phone: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    preop_exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 术式组
    refractive_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    operation_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    operation_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128))
    operation_level: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False, default="OU")
    is_intraocular: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    is_enhancement: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    plan_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False, default="NEW")
    previous_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 医师与机构组
    dept_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    dept_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    chief_doctor_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    chief_doctor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    planner_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    planner_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    auth_check_passed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    auth_check_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    auth_check_result: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 状态组
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False, default="DRAFT")
    submitted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    audited_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    reject_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    expire_at: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    cancelled_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    superseded_by_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    current_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)

    # 安全判定汇总组
    ablation_feasible: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    min_rsb_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    rsb_redline_violated: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    risk_level: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    contraindication_hit: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    consent_required: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    consent_signed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    # 费用快照组（M16 返回，M11 不计算）
    fee_estimate_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4))
    fee_estimate_at_snap: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    fee_detail_snap: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    discount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4))

    # 备注与标准字段
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    internal_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_plan_no", "plan_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("plan_no IS NOT NULL AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_patient", "patient_id", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_patient_active", "patient_id",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_deleted = FALSE AND status IN "
                  "('DRAFT', 'PENDING_AUDIT', 'AUDITED')")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_pending_audit", "submitted_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "status = 'PENDING_AUDIT' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_expire", "expire_at",
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'AUDITED' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_status", "status", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_type", "refractive_type", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_doctor", "chief_doctor_id", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_rsb", "rsb_redline_violated",
              postgresql_where=text("rsb_redline_violated = TRUE")),
        CheckConstraint("plan_no IS NULL OR plan_no ~ '^FA[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status = 'DRAFT' OR plan_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_no_required"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('DRAFT', 'PENDING_AUDIT', 'AUDITED', 'REJECTED', "
            "'EXPIRED', 'CANCELLED', 'SUPERSEDED')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refractive_type IN ('SMILE', 'FS_LASIK', 'TRANSPRK', 'SMART', "
            "'ICL', 'PRL', 'CXL', 'PIOL', 'RK', 'OTHER_REFRACTIVE')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_refractive_type"),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS', 'OU', 'NA')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "plan_source IN ('NEW', 'RE_TREATMENT', 'ENHANCEMENT', 'OTHERS_HOSPITAL')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_source"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "risk_level IS NULL OR risk_level IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'FORBIDDEN')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_risk_level"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(refractive_type IN ('ICL', 'PRL', 'PIOL') AND is_intraocular = TRUE) "
            "OR (refractive_type NOT IN ('ICL', 'PRL', 'PIOL') "
            "AND is_intraocular = FALSE)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_intraocular"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_intraocular = FALSE OR (ablation_feasible IS NULL "
            "AND min_rsb_um IS NULL AND rsb_redline_violated = FALSE)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_nonablation"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(rsb_redline_violated = TRUE AND min_rsb_um IS NOT NULL "
            "AND min_rsb_um < 250) "
            "OR (rsb_redline_violated = FALSE "
            "AND (min_rsb_um IS NULL OR min_rsb_um >= 250))",
            name="ck_m11_plan_rsb_redline"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'REJECTED' OR (reject_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(reject_reason)) >= 10)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_rejected"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'CANCELLED' OR (cancel_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(cancel_reason)) >= 10 "
            "AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_cancelled"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'SUPERSEDED' OR superseded_by_plan_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_plan_superseded"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status NOT IN ('AUDITED', 'SUPERSEDED', 'EXPIRED') "
            "OR (audited_at IS NOT NULL AND expire_at IS NOT NULL "
            "AND auth_check_passed = TRUE)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audited"),
        CheckConstraint("status = 'REJECTED' OR reject_reason IS NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_reject_only"),
        CheckConstraint("age IS NULL OR age BETWEEN 1 AND 120",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_age"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "plan_source IN ('NEW', 'OTHERS_HOSPITAL') "
            "OR previous_plan_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_plan_previous_required"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_enhancement = FALSE OR plan_source = 'ENHANCEMENT'",
            name="ck_m11_plan_enhancement"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(fee_estimate_snap IS NULL OR fee_estimate_snap >= 0) "
            "AND (discount_snap IS NULL OR discount_snap >= 0)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_fee"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "consent_signed = FALSE "
            "OR status IN ('AUDITED', 'SUPERSEDED', 'EXPIRED')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_consent"),
        CheckConstraint("plan_no IS NULL OR is_deleted = FALSE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_no_survive"),
    )


class M11PlanPreopSnap(Base):
    """方案术前基线快照表。方案订立时点冻结，1:1 且不可变。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_preop_snap"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)

    preop_exam_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    preop_exam_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    exam_completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    admission_result: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    snap_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="AUTO")
    snapped_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    snapped_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)

    # 屈光基线（OD/OS 各一组）
    od_sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    od_ucva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2))
    od_bcva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2))
    od_add: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    os_ucva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2))
    os_bcva: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(4, 2))
    os_add: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 角膜形态
    od_k1: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_k2: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_km: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_axis_k2: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    od_cornea_astig: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_k1: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_k2: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_km: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_axis_k2: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    os_cornea_astig: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k_unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False, default="D")

    # 角膜厚度
    od_cct: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_thinnest: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_thinnest_loc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    os_cct: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_thinnest: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_thinnest_loc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 眼轴与前房
    od_axial_length: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_axial_length: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_acd: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_acd: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_wtw: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_wtw: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_lt: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_lt: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 眼压与内皮
    od_iop: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1))
    os_iop: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1))
    od_endothelial_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    os_endothelial_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)

    # 瞳孔与高阶像差
    od_pupil_scotopic: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    os_pupil_scotopic: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    od_pupil_photopic: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    os_pupil_photopic: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    od_hoa_rms: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))
    os_hoa_rms: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))

    # 眼表与地形图
    od_tbtt: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1))
    os_tbtt: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 1))
    od_topo_pattern: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    os_topo_pattern: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    keratoconus_suspect: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    od_topo_report_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    os_topo_report_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)

    # 既往史
    previous_refractive_surgery: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    previous_surgery_detail: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    has_dry_eye: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    has_autoimmune: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    has_glaucoma: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    pregnant: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    contact_lens_stop_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    rigid_lens_stop_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)

    # 完整快照
    full_snapshot: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False)
    snapshot_hash: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_plan_preop_snap_plan", "plan_id", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_preop_snap_patient", "patient_id",
              text("snapped_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_preop_snap_kc", "patient_id",
              postgresql_where=text("keratoconus_suspect = TRUE")),
        CheckConstraint("snap_source IN ('AUTO', 'MANUAL')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_source"),
        CheckConstraint("k_unit IN ('D', 'MM')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_kunit"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "admission_result IS NULL OR admission_result IN "
            "('PASS', 'HOLD', 'REJECT', 'PENDING')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_admission"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_k1 IS NULL OR od_k2 IS NULL OR od_k1 <= od_k2) "
            "AND (os_k1 IS NULL OR os_k2 IS NULL OR os_k1 <= os_k2)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_k_order"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_cct IS NULL OR od_cct BETWEEN 300.00 AND 900.00) "
            "AND (os_cct IS NULL OR os_cct BETWEEN 300.00 AND 900.00)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_cct"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_thinnest IS NULL OR od_cct IS NULL OR od_thinnest <= od_cct) "
            "AND (os_thinnest IS NULL OR os_cct IS NULL OR os_thinnest <= os_cct)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_thinnest"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_axial_length IS NULL OR od_axial_length BETWEEN 18.00 AND 36.00) "
            "AND (os_axial_length IS NULL OR os_axial_length BETWEEN 18.00 AND 36.00)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_axial"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_acd IS NULL OR od_acd BETWEEN 1.50 AND 6.00) "
            "AND (os_acd IS NULL OR os_acd BETWEEN 1.50 AND 6.00)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_acd"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_wtw IS NULL OR od_wtw BETWEEN 9.00 AND 14.00) "
            "AND (os_wtw IS NULL OR os_wtw BETWEEN 9.00 AND 14.00)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_wtw"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_iop IS NULL OR od_iop BETWEEN 5.0 AND 40.0) "
            "AND (os_iop IS NULL OR os_iop BETWEEN 5.0 AND 40.0)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_iop"),
        CheckConstraint("jsonb_typeof(full_snapshot) = 'object'",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_preop_snap_full"),
    )


class M11PlanAblation(Base):
    """角膜切削参数表。按方案 × 眼别存最终 OZ/TZ/切削深度/RSB。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_ablation"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    refractive_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)

    # 输入参数
    pre_sphere: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    pre_cylinder: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    pre_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    pre_se: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    target_sphere: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"))
    target_cylinder: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"))
    cct_um: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    thinnest_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k1: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    k2: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    km: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    pupil_scotopic: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))

    # 光学区与过渡区
    oz_mm: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=False)
    tz_mm: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=False)
    tz_width_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    oz_selected_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="AUTO")
    oz_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))

    # 切削深度
    ablation_max_um: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    ablation_center_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    ablation_total_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    ablation_optical_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    ablation_transition_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    flap_thickness_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cap_thickness_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 剩余基质床（核心安全）
    rsb_um: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    rsb_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING")
    rsb_redline_um: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("250.00"))
    thickness_margin_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    rsb_source_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 其他设备参数
    optical_zone_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="ASPIRATION")
    treatment_astig: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    treatment_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    nomogram_used: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    nomogram_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    nomogram_adjust_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    algo_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    algo_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)

    # 可行性判定
    feasible: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    infeasible_reasons: Mapped[Optional[List[Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    warn_flags: Mapped[Optional[List[Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 留痕
    confirm_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    confirm_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_plan_ablation_eye", "plan_id", "eye_side", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_violation", "rsb_status",
              text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("rsb_status = 'VIOLATION'")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_warn", "rsb_um",
              postgresql_where=text("rsb_status = 'WARN'")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_ablation_infeasible", "plan_id",
              postgresql_where=text("feasible = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refractive_type IN ('SMILE', 'FS_LASIK', 'TRANSPRK', 'SMART')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_type"),
        # ★ RSB 红线：绝不小于 250μm
        CheckConstraint("rsb_um >= 250.00", name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_redline"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(rsb_status = 'VIOLATION' AND rsb_um < 250.00) "
            "OR (rsb_status = 'WARN' AND rsb_um >= 250.00 AND rsb_um < 300.00) "
            "OR (rsb_status = 'PASS' AND rsb_um >= 300.00) "
            "OR (rsb_status = 'PENDING')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_status"),
        CheckConstraint("rsb_redline_um >= 250.00",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_threshold"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "rsb_source_um IS NULL OR abs(rsb_um - (rsb_source_um "
            "- COALESCE(flap_thickness_um, cap_thickness_um, 0) "
            "- ablation_max_um)) <= 0.01",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_formula"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "NOT (flap_thickness_um IS NOT NULL AND cap_thickness_um IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_flap_cap"),
        CheckConstraint("oz_mm BETWEEN 5.50 AND 8.00", name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_oz"),
        CheckConstraint("tz_mm >= oz_mm AND tz_mm <= 9.50",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_tz"),
        CheckConstraint("ablation_max_um > 0 AND ablation_max_um <= 200.00",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_depth"),
        CheckConstraint("cct_um BETWEEN 300.00 AND 900.00",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_cct"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(pre_cylinder = 0 AND pre_axis IS NULL) "
            "OR (pre_cylinder <> 0 AND pre_axis IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_axis_pair"),
        CheckConstraint("feasible = TRUE OR (infeasible_reasons IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND jsonb_array_length(infeasible_reasons) > 0)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_infeasible"),
        CheckConstraint("rsb_status <> 'VIOLATION' OR feasible = FALSE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_violation"),
        CheckConstraint("rsb_status <> 'PENDING' OR feasible = FALSE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_pending"),
    )


class M11PlanAblationCalc(Base):
    """切削计算过程留痕表。一次计算一行，含公式/中间值/输出/采纳标记。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_ablation_calc"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    calc_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(40))
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    calc_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)

    algo_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    algo_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    refractive_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)

    pre_sphere: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    pre_cylinder: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    pre_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    target_sphere: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"))
    target_cylinder: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"))
    oz_mm: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=False)
    tz_mm: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=False)
    cct_um: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    thinnest_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    flap_thickness_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    nomogram_adjust_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    inputs_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    diopter_correction_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    sphere_component_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cylinder_component_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    sa_correction_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    iteration_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    converged: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)

    ablation_max_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    ablation_center_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    ablation_total_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    rsb_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    rsb_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    outputs_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    feasible: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    infeasible_reasons: Mapped[Optional[List[Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    warn_flags: Mapped[Optional[List[Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    is_adopted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    adopted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    calc_duration_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    calc_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    calc_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    calc_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_plan_ablation_calc_seq", "plan_id", "eye_side", "calc_seq",
              unique=True),
        Index("uk_m11_plan_ablation_calc_adopted", "plan_id", "eye_side",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_adopted = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_ablation_calc_algo", "algo_code", "algo_version",
              text("calc_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_ablation_calc_violation", "rsb_status",
              postgresql_where=text("rsb_status = 'VIOLATION'")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_eye"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_seq BETWEEN 1 AND 999",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_seq"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "algo_code IN ('MUNNERLYN', 'MROCHEN', 'TRANS_ASTIG', "
            "'TOPO_GUIDED', 'WAVEFRONT')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_algo"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refractive_type IN ('SMILE', 'FS_LASIK', 'TRANSPRK', 'SMART')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "rsb_status IS NULL OR rsb_status IN ('PASS', 'WARN', 'VIOLATION', 'PENDING')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_rsb_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "ablation_max_um IS NULL OR (ablation_max_um > 0 AND ablation_max_um <= 200.00)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_max"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "feasible = TRUE OR (infeasible_reasons IS NOT NULL "
            "AND jsonb_array_length(infeasible_reasons) > 0)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_infeasible"),
        CheckConstraint("is_adopted = FALSE OR adopted_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_adopted_ck"),
        CheckConstraint("rsb_status <> 'VIOLATION' OR feasible = FALSE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_violation_ck"),
        CheckConstraint("oz_mm BETWEEN 5.50 AND 8.00 AND tz_mm >= oz_mm",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_ablation_calc_oz"),
    )


class M11IolCalc(Base):
    """晶体度数计算表。ICL/PRL/PIOL 晶体度数计算，P-01 契约数据源。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_iol_calc"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    calc_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(40))
    plan_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    patient_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    record_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    refractive_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False, default="OU")
    iol_model: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    iol_material_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    iol_manufacturer: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    # 计算公式组（★选择理由必填）
    formula_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    formula_reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=False)
    formula_selected_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    vendor_nomogram_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    formula_version: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))

    # 生物测量输入
    axial_length_mm: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    k1: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    k2: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    km: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    anterior_chamber_depth_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    lens_thickness_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    white_to_white_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    refraction_sphere: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    refraction_cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    refraction_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    astigmatism_target: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="TORIC_NONE")

    # 输出（理论度数）
    theoretical_power_d: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    theoretical_sphere_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    theoretical_cylinder_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    theoretical_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    theoretical_se_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    pred_refraction_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    # 选择与偏差（★M09 核对依据）
    selected_power_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    selected_sphere_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    selected_cylinder_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    selected_axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    deviation_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    deviation_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING")
    deviation_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    deviation_tolerance_d: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.25"))

    # 尺寸与库存
    iol_size_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    iol_size_rule: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    vault_estimate_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    material_available: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    material_available_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    material_snap: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 状态与确认
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="DRAFT")
    is_current: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    confirmed_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    confirmed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    calc_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    calc_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    calc_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())

    inputs_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    outputs_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    calc_duration_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_iol_calc_no", "calc_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("calc_no IS NOT NULL AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        # ★ P-01 主力索引：患者当前有效计算唯一
        Index("uk_m11_iol_calc_current", "patient_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_current = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_iol_calc_patient", "patient_id", text("calc_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_iol_calc_formula", "formula_code", text("calc_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_iol_calc_deviation", "deviation_status", text("calc_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "deviation_status IN ('EXCEED', 'JUSTIFIED')")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS', 'OU')",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_eye"),
        CheckConstraint("refractive_type IN ('ICL', 'PRL', 'PIOL')",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_type"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('DRAFT', 'CONFIRMED', 'SUPERSEDED', 'CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "formula_code IN ('SRK_T', 'HOLLADAY_1', 'HOLLADAY_2', 'HAIGIS', "
            "'BARRETT_UNIVERSAL_II', 'VENDOR_NOMOGRAM')",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_formula"),
        # ★ 公式选择理由必填且不少于 10 字
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(formula_reason)) >= 10",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_reason"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "formula_code <> 'VENDOR_NOMOGRAM' OR vendor_nomogram_code IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_nomogram"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "deviation_status IN ('PENDING', 'WITHIN_TOLERANCE', 'EXCEED', 'JUSTIFIED')",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_dev_status"),
        CheckConstraint("deviation_tolerance_d > 0 AND deviation_tolerance_d <= 1.00",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_tolerance"),
        # ★ 偏差必须等于 选择 − 理论
        CheckConstraint(
            "selected_power_d IS NULL OR deviation_d IS NULL "
            "OR abs(deviation_d - (selected_power_d - theoretical_power_d)) <= 0.001",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_deviation"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(deviation_status = 'PENDING' "
            " AND (selected_power_d IS NULL OR deviation_d IS NULL)) "
            "OR (deviation_status = 'WITHIN_TOLERANCE' AND deviation_d IS NOT NULL "
            "    AND abs(deviation_d) <= deviation_tolerance_d) "
            "OR (deviation_status IN ('EXCEED', 'JUSTIFIED') "
            "    AND deviation_d IS NOT NULL "
            "    AND abs(deviation_d) > deviation_tolerance_d)",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_dev_consistent"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "deviation_status <> 'JUSTIFIED' OR (deviation_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(deviation_reason)) >= 10)",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_justified"),
        CheckConstraint("theoretical_power_d BETWEEN -30.00 AND 40.00",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_power"),
        CheckConstraint("axial_length_mm BETWEEN 18.00 AND 36.00",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_al"),
        CheckConstraint("k1 BETWEEN 30.00 AND 70.00 AND k2 BETWEEN 30.00 AND 70.00 "
                        "AND k1 <= k2", name="ck_m11_iol_calc_k"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "anterior_chamber_depth_mm IS NULL "
            "OR anterior_chamber_depth_mm BETWEEN 1.50 AND 6.00",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_acd"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "astigmatism_target IN ('TORIC_NONE', 'TORIC_FULL', 'TORIC_PARTIAL')",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_astig_target"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status NOT IN ('CONFIRMED', 'SUPERSEDED') "
            "OR (confirmed_by IS NOT NULL AND confirmed_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_confirmed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CONFIRMED' OR selected_power_d IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_confirmed_selected"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(material_available IS NULL AND material_available_at IS NULL) "
            "OR (material_available IS NOT NULL AND material_available_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_material"),
    )


class M11IolCalcDetail(Base):
    """晶体计算结果明细表。一次计算的每个公式各一条结果。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_iol_calc_detail"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    iol_calc_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    calc_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(40))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    formula_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    formula_version: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    is_primary: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    axial_length_mm: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    k1: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    k2: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    km: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    anterior_chamber_depth_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    lens_thickness_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    white_to_white_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    lens_power_d: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=False)
    lens_sphere_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    lens_cylinder_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    pred_refraction_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    pred_refraction_se_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))

    effective_iol_position: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))
    corneal_height_k: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))
    corneal_radius: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))
    predicted_acd: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))
    a_constant: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))
    surgeon_factor: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 3))

    deviation_from_primary_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    is_outlier: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    warning_message: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    calculation_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_iol_calc_detail_formula", "iol_calc_id", "eye_side",
              "formula_code", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_iol_calc_detail_primary", "iol_calc_id", "eye_side",
              unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_primary = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_iol_calc_detail_formula_stat", "formula_code",
              text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS')",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "formula_code IN ('SRK_T', 'HOLLADAY_1', 'HOLLADAY_2', 'HAIGIS', "
            "'BARRETT_UNIVERSAL_II', 'VENDOR_NOMOGRAM')",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_formula_ck"),
        CheckConstraint("lens_power_d BETWEEN -30.00 AND 40.00",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_power"),
        CheckConstraint("axial_length_mm BETWEEN 18.00 AND 36.00",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_al"),
        CheckConstraint("k1 BETWEEN 30.00 AND 70.00 AND k2 BETWEEN 30.00 AND 70.00 "
                        "AND k1 <= k2", name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_k"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_outlier = FALSE OR (deviation_from_primary_d IS NOT NULL "
            "AND abs(deviation_from_primary_d) > 0.75)",
            name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_outlier"),
        CheckConstraint("is_primary = FALSE OR deviation_from_primary_d IS NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_primary_ck"),
        CheckConstraint("effective_iol_position IS NULL "
                        "OR effective_iol_position BETWEEN 3.00 AND 8.00",
                        name="ck_m11_iol_calc_detail_elp"),
    )


class M11PlanAudit(Base):
    """方案审核表。审核流每次动作一条，二级/三级审核留痕。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_audit"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    audit_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(40))
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)

    audit_action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    audit_level: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    required_levels: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=2)
    audit_result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING")
    from_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    to_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))

    auditor_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    auditor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    auditor_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    auditor_title: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    audit_opinion: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    reject_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    reject_items: Mapped[Optional[List[Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    checklist_result: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    checklist_passed_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    checklist_total_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)

    auth_rechecked: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    auth_check_passed: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    auth_check_result: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    auth_checked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    submitted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    audit_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    duration_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    audit_deadline: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    is_overdue: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_m11_plan_audit_plan", "plan_id", text("audit_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_audit_pending", "audit_level", "submitted_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "audit_result = 'PENDING' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_audit_auditor", "auditor_id", text("audit_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_audit_overdue", "audit_deadline",
              postgresql_where=text("is_overdue = TRUE")),
        CheckConstraint(
            "audit_action IN ('SUBMIT', 'AUDIT', 'APPROVE', 'REJECT', 'WITHDRAW')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_action"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_result IN ('PENDING', 'PASSED', 'REJECTED')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_audit_result"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_level BETWEEN 1 AND 3",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_audit_level"),
        CheckConstraint("required_levels IN (2, 3)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_audit_required"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_level <= required_levels",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_audit_level_le"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "auditor_role IS NULL OR auditor_role IN "
            "('CHIEF_DOCTOR', 'SENIOR_DOCTOR', 'QC', 'MEDICAL_AFFAIRS')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_role"),
        # ★ 驳回必须有原因与问题项
        CheckConstraint(
            "audit_result <> 'REJECTED' OR (reject_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(reject_reason)) >= 10 "
            "AND reject_items IS NOT NULL "
            "AND jsonb_array_length(reject_items) > 0)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_reject"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(audit_action = 'REJECT' AND audit_result = 'REJECTED') "
            "OR (audit_action <> 'REJECT' AND audit_result <> 'REJECTED')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_reject_action"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_action <> 'SUBMIT' OR submitted_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_audit_submit"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "audit_action IN ('SUBMIT', 'WITHDRAW') "
            "OR (auditor_id IS NOT NULL AND auditor_name IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_auditor"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "audit_action NOT IN ('AUDIT', 'APPROVE') "
            "OR (checklist_result IS NOT NULL AND checklist_total_count IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_checklist"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "auth_rechecked = FALSE AND auth_checked_at IS NULL "
            "OR (auth_rechecked = TRUE AND auth_checked_at IS NOT NULL "
            "    AND auth_check_passed IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_auth"),
        CheckConstraint("is_overdue = FALSE OR audit_deadline IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_audit_overdue_ck"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(from_status IS NULL AND to_status IS NULL) "
            "OR (from_status IS NOT NULL AND to_status IS NOT NULL "
            "    AND from_status <> to_status)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_audit_status_pair"),
    )


class M11PlanVersion(Base):
    """方案版本表。每次提交审核生成一个完整不可变版本快照。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_version"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    version_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)

    refractive_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    chief_doctor_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    chief_doctor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    snapshot_json: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False)
    snapshot_hash: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    previous_version_no: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    diff_summary: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    diff_field_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    change_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    changed_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    changed_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    od_oz_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    os_oz_mm: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    od_ablation_max_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_ablation_max_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_rsb_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_rsb_um: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    od_iol_power_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    os_iol_power_d: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    risk_level: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))

    is_current: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    is_audited: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    audited_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_plan_version_no", "plan_id", "version_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_plan_version_current", "plan_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_current = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_version_plan", "plan_id", text("version_no DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_version_risk", "risk_level", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("version_no BETWEEN 1 AND 999",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_version_range"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "refractive_type IN ('SMILE', 'FS_LASIK', 'TRANSPRK', 'SMART', "
            "'ICL', 'PRL', 'CXL', 'PIOL', 'RK', 'OTHER_REFRACTIVE')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_version_type"),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS', 'OU', 'NA')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_version_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "risk_level IS NULL OR risk_level IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'FORBIDDEN')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_version_risk_ck"),
        CheckConstraint("jsonb_typeof(snapshot_json) = 'object'",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_version_snapshot"),
        # 首版无上一版；非首版必须指向上一版且写修订原因
        CheckConstraint(
            "(version_no = 1 AND previous_version_no IS NULL) "
            "OR (version_no > 1 AND previous_version_no = version_no - 1 "
            "    AND change_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "    AND char_length(btrim(change_reason)) >= 10)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_version_prev"),
        CheckConstraint("is_audited = FALSE OR audited_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_version_audited"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_rsb_um IS NULL OR od_rsb_um > 0) "
            "AND (os_rsb_um IS NULL OR os_rsb_um > 0)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_version_rsb"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(od_oz_mm IS NULL OR od_oz_mm BETWEEN 5.50 AND 8.00) "
            "AND (os_oz_mm IS NULL OR os_oz_mm BETWEEN 5.50 AND 8.00)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_version_oz"),
    )


class M11PlanRisk(Base):
    """方案风险提示表。每个方案命中的风险项，含阻断级与提示级。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_risk"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    risk_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(40))
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False, default="NA")

    risk_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    risk_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    risk_category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    risk_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    is_blocking: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    trigger_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(10, 2))
    trigger_value_str: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    threshold_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(10, 2))
    threshold_op: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8))
    trigger_unit: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    rule_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    rule_version: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))

    risk_desc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    patient_desc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    suggestion: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    alternative_hint: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))

    acknowledged: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    acknowledged_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    acknowledged_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    acknowledged_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    ack_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
```
