```python
    patient_notified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    patient_notified_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    resolved: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    resolved_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    resolved_by_change: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_m11_plan_risk_plan", "plan_id", "risk_level",
              text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_plan_risk_item", "plan_id", "eye_side", "risk_code",
              unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_active = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_risk_blocking", "plan_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_blocking = TRUE AND is_active = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_risk_unack", "plan_id",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "is_blocking = TRUE AND acknowledged = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_risk_code", "risk_code", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD', 'OS', 'OU', 'NA')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_eye"),
        CheckConstraint("risk_level IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'FORBIDDEN')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_level"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "risk_category IN ('ABLATION_SAFETY', 'CORNEAL', 'OCULAR_SURFACE', "
            "'OPTICAL_QUALITY', 'INTRAOCULAR', 'CONTRAINDICATION', 'PROCEDURAL')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_risk_category"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "threshold_op IS NULL "
            "OR threshold_op IN ('LT', 'LTE', 'GT', 'GTE', 'EQ', 'NE')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_risk_op"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "trigger_unit IS NULL OR trigger_unit IN ('μm', 'mm', 'D', '%', 'NA')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_risk_unit"),
        # ★ FORBIDDEN 级必须阻断
        CheckConstraint("risk_level <> 'FORBIDDEN' OR is_blocking = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_blocking_ck"),
        # ★ 阻断级必须已告知患者或已消除
        CheckConstraint("is_blocking = FALSE OR resolved = TRUE "
                        "OR patient_notified = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_notify"),
        CheckConstraint("acknowledged = FALSE OR (acknowledged_by IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND acknowledged_at IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_ack"),
        CheckConstraint("patient_notified = FALSE OR patient_notified_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_notify_pair"),
        CheckConstraint("resolved = FALSE OR resolved_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_resolved"),
        CheckConstraint("trigger_value IS NOT NULL OR trigger_value_str IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_trigger"),
        CheckConstraint("trigger_value IS NULL "
                        "OR (threshold_value IS NOT NULL AND threshold_op IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_risk_threshold"),
    )


class M11PlanConsentSnap(Base):
    """知情同意方案快照表。签署时点方案冻结，固化后不可变。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_consent_snap"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    consent_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(40))
    plan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    plan_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)

    consent_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, default="SURGERY")
    consent_template_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    consent_template_version: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    snapshot_json: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False)
    snapshot_hash: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    risk_codes_snapshot: Mapped[List[Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False)
    risk_count: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)

    signer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    signer_relation: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="SELF")
    signer_id_card: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    signed_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    witness_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    witness_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)

    inform_doctor_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    inform_doctor_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    informed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    inform_duration_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    qa_record: Mapped[Optional[List[Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    pdf_file_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    pdf_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    signature_image_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    scan_file_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)

    is_sealed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    sealed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    is_revoked: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    revoked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    revoke_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    revoke_witness: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m11_plan_consent_snap_plan", "plan_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m11_plan_consent_snap_no", "consent_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("consent_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_consent_snap_patient", "patient_id",
              text("signed_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_consent_snap_unsealed", "signed_at",
              postgresql_where=text("is_sealed = FALSE AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint(
            "consent_type IN ('SURGERY', 'ANESTHESIA', 'IOL_IMPLANT', 'RE_TREATMENT')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_consent_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "signer_relation IN ('SELF', 'FATHER', 'MOTHER', 'GUARDIAN', 'OTHER')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_consent_relation"),
        CheckConstraint("jsonb_typeof(snapshot_json) = 'object'",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_snapshot"),
        CheckConstraint("jsonb_typeof(risk_codes_snapshot) = 'array'",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_risks"),
        CheckConstraint("signer_relation = 'SELF' OR witness_name IS NOT NULL "
                        "OR signer_id_card IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_signer"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "inform_duration_minutes IS NULL "
            "OR inform_duration_minutes BETWEEN 1 AND 240",
            name="ck_m11_plan_consent_duration"),
        CheckConstraint("is_sealed = FALSE OR sealed_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_sealed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_sealed = FALSE OR (pdf_file_id IS NOT NULL AND snapshot_hash IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_consent_sealed_files"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_revoked = FALSE OR (revoked_at IS NOT NULL AND revoke_reason IS NOT NULL "
            "AND char_length(btrim(revoke_reason)) >= 10)",
            name="ck_m11_plan_consent_revoked"),
        CheckConstraint("is_revoked = FALSE "
                        "OR (is_sealed = TRUE AND sealed_at <= revoked_at)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_revoke_order"),
        CheckConstraint("informed_at IS NULL OR signed_at >= informed_at",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_order"),
        # ★ 已固化记录不可逻辑删除（证据保全）
        CheckConstraint("is_sealed = FALSE OR is_deleted = FALSE",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_consent_immutable"),
    )


class M11PlanLog(Base):
    """方案操作日志表。28 个动作全留痕，按月 RANGE 分区。"""

    __tablename__ = "m11_plan_log"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    log_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(48))
    plan_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    entity_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    entity_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=False)
    action_desc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    from_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    to_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    changed_fields: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    operator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    dept_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    client_ip: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    user_agent: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="SUCCESS")
    error_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    error_message: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    duration_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.now())

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_m11_plan_log_plan", "plan_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_log_entity", "entity_type", "entity_id",
              text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_log_action", "action", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_log_operator", "operator_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m11_plan_log_fail", "created_at",
              postgresql_where=text("result = 'FAIL'")),
        CheckConstraint("log_no IS NULL OR log_no ~ '^PLG[0-9]{20}$'",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_log_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "entity_type IN ('PLAN', 'PREOP_SNAP', 'ABLATION', 'ABLATION_CALC', "
            "'IOL_CALC', 'AUDIT', 'VERSION', 'RISK', 'CONSENT')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_log_entity"),
        CheckConstraint("result IN ('SUCCESS', 'FAIL')",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_log_result"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "operator_role IS NULL OR operator_role IN "
            "('DOCTOR', 'CHIEF_DOCTOR', 'QC', 'NURSE', 'SYSTEM')",
            name="ck_m11_plan_log_role"),
        CheckConstraint("result = 'SUCCESS' OR (error_code IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND error_message IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_log_fail_ck"),
        CheckConstraint("result = 'FAIL' OR (error_code IS NULL "
                        "AND error_message IS NULL)",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_log_success"),
        CheckConstraint("entity_type IN ('PLAN', 'RISK') OR plan_id IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m11_plan_log_plan_ref"),
    )
```

---
### 4.14 Pydantic Schema 定义

```python
# app/schemas/m11/plan.py
"""M11-04 手术方案与切削计算 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

RefractiveType = Literal[
    "SMILE", "FS_LASIK", "TRANSPRK", "SMART", "ICL",
    "PRL", "CXL", "PIOL", "RK", "OTHER_REFRACTIVE",
]
EyeSide = Literal["OD", "OS", "OU", "NA"]
PlanStatus = Literal[
    "DRAFT", "PENDING_AUDIT", "AUDITED", "REJECTED",
    "EXPIRED", "CANCELLED", "SUPERSEDED",
]
PlanSource = Literal["NEW", "RE_TREATMENT", "ENHANCEMENT", "OTHERS_HOSPITAL"]
RiskLevel = Literal["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH", "FORBIDDEN"]
RsbStatus = Literal["PASS", "WARN", "VIOLATION", "PENDING"]
IolFormula = Literal[
    "SRK_T", "HOLLADAY_1", "HOLLADAY_2", "HAIGIS",
    "BARRETT_UNIVERSAL_II", "VENDOR_NOMOGRAM",
]
DeviationStatus = Literal["PENDING", "WITHIN_TOLERANCE", "EXCEED", "JUSTIFIED"]
AblationAlgo = Literal[
    "MUNNERLYN", "MROCHEN", "TRANS_ASTIG", "TOPO_GUIDED", "WAVEFRONT",
]


# ==================== 请求体 ====================

class PlanCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """开立手术方案。"""

    patient_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="患者 ID（M03 mpi_patient.id）")
    preop_exam_no: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=32, description="术前检查单号（M11-02，用于自动拉取快照）")
    record_no: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=32, description="屈光档案号（M11-03，只读引用）")
    refractive_type: RefractiveType = Field(..., description="屈光术式码（M01-07 10 个之一）")
    operation_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="手术项目编码（M01-07）")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field("OU", description="手术眼别 OD/OS/OU")
    plan_source: PlanSource = Field("NEW", description="方案来源")
    previous_plan_no: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=32, description="上一次手术方案号（再手术/增效必填）")
    chief_doctor_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="主刀医师 ID（M01-02）")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="手术科室 ID（M01-01）")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_previous_and_source(self) -> "PlanCreateIn":
        """再手术与增效必须指向原方案。"""
        if self.plan_source in ("RE_TREATMENT", "ENHANCEMENT"):
            if not self.previous_plan_no:
                raise ValueError("再手术或增效方案必须填写 previous_plan_no")
        return self


class PlanUpdateIn(BaseModel):
    """修改方案基础信息（草稿状态）。"""

    chief_doctor_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主刀医师 ID")
    eye_side: Optional[EyeSide] = Field(None, description="手术眼别")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")
    internal_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="内部备注")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")


class AblationCalcIn(BaseModel):
    """角膜切削参数计算请求（P04）。"""

    plan_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="方案 ID")
    eye_side: Literal["OD", "OS"] = Field(..., description="计算眼别（单眼）")
    oz_mm: Decimal = Field(..., ge=5.50, le=8.00, description="光学区直径 OZ（mm）")
    tz_mm: Decimal = Field(..., ge=5.50, le=9.50, description="过渡区外径 TZ（mm）")
    flap_thickness_um: Optional[Decimal] = Field(
        None, ge=80, le=160, description="瓣/帽厚度（μm），FS_LASIK/SMILE 必填")
    algo_code: AblationAlgo = Field("MUNNERLYN", description="计算算法")
    nomogram_adjust_d: Optional[Decimal] = Field(
        None, ge=-2.00, le=2.00, description="nomogram 修正量（D）")
    optical_zone_type: Literal[
        "STANDARD", "ASPIRATION", "TOPO_GUIDED", "WAVEFRONT"
    ] = Field("ASPIRATION", description="切削模式")
    target_sphere: Decimal = Field(
        Decimal("0.00"), ge=-10, le=10, description="目标球镜（D）")
    oz_note: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=256, description="OZ 选择说明（手工指定时必填）")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_tz_and_oz(self) -> "AblationCalcIn":
        """TZ 必须大于等于 OZ；TZ 宽度不超过设备上限。"""
        if self.tz_mm < self.oz_mm:
            raise ValueError("过渡区外径 TZ 必须大于等于光学区直径 OZ")
        if (self.tz_mm - self.oz_mm) / 2 < 0.80:
            raise ValueError("过渡区宽度不足 0.80mm，无法形成平滑过渡")
        return self


class IolCalcIn(BaseModel):
    """晶体度数计算请求（P05）。"""

    patient_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="患者 ID")
    plan_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="方案 ID（可空）")
    refractive_type: Literal["ICL", "PRL", "PIOL"] = Field(..., description="眼内屈光术式码")
    eye_side: Literal["OD", "OS", "OU"] = Field("OU", description="计算眼别")
    formula_code: IolFormula = Field(..., description="主用公式编码")
    formula_reason: str = Field(
        ..., min_length=10, max_length=512,
        description="公式选择理由（不少于 10 字，如「眼轴 25.4mm 在 22~26mm 区间，SRK/T 误差最小」）")
    vendor_nomogram_code: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=32, description="厂商 nomogram 编码（formula_code=VENDOR_NOMOGRAM 时必填）")
    ial_models: List[IolFormula] = Field(
        default_factory=list, description="并行计算的交叉验证公式列表")
    iol_model: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="晶体型号")
    iol_material_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="晶体物料编码（M01-04）")
    astigmatism_target: Literal[
        "TORIC_NONE", "TORIC_FULL", "TORIC_PARTIAL"
    ] = Field("TORIC_NONE", description="散光矫正目标")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_nomogram(self) -> "IolCalcIn":
        """厂商 nomogram 必须提供编码。"""
        if self.formula_code == "VENDOR_NOMOGRAM" and not self.vendor_nomogram_code:
            raise ValueError("formula_code 为 VENDOR_NOMOGRAM 时必须填写 vendor_nomogram_code")
        return self


class IolSelectIn(BaseModel):
    """确认实际选择度数（供 M09 核对）。"""

    selected_power_d: Decimal = Field(
        ..., ge=-30, le=40, description="医师实际选择度数（D）")
    selected_sphere_d: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=-30, le=40, description="选择球镜（D）")
    selected_cylinder_d: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=-8, le=8, description="选择柱镜（D）")
    selected_axis: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=180, description="选择轴位（°）")
    deviation_reason: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=512, description="偏差超阈值时的说明（不少于 10 字）")
    iol_size_mm: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=11, le=16, description="晶体尺寸（mm）")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_toric(self) -> "IolSelectIn":
        """选择环曲面晶体必须给轴位。"""
        if self.selected_cylinder_d is not None and self.selected_axis is None:
            raise ValueError("选择柱镜非 0 时必须填写轴位")
        return self


class PlanAuditIn(BaseModel):
    """方案审核请求（P06）。"""

    audit_action: Literal["AUDIT", "APPROVE", "REJECT"] = Field(..., description="审核动作")
    audit_result: Literal["PASSED", "REJECTED"] = Field(..., description="审核结果")
    audit_opinion: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="审核意见")
    reject_reason: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=512, description="驳回原因（驳回时必填，不少于 10 字）")
    reject_items: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, description="驳回问题项编码数组")
    checklist_result: Optional[Dict[str, bool]] = Field(
        None, description="审核检查表 11 项逐项结果")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_reject(self) -> "PlanAuditIn":
        """驳回必须写原因与问题项；动作与结果必须匹配。"""
        if self.audit_action == "REJECT":
            if self.audit_result != "REJECTED":
                raise ValueError("动作 REJECT 必须对应结果 REJECTED")
            if not self.reject_reason or len(self.reject_reason.strip()) < 10:
                raise ValueError("驳回必须填写原因（不少于 10 字）")
            if not self.reject_items:
                raise ValueError("驳回必须至少指定一个驳回问题项")
        elif self.audit_result == "REJECTED":
            raise ValueError("结果 REJECTED 必须由动作 REJECT 产生")
        return self


class RiskAckIn(BaseModel):
    """风险确认与患者告知（P08）。"""

    risk_ids: List[int] = Field(..., min_length=1, description="待确认的风险记录 ID 列表")
    ack_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="确认备注")
    patient_notified: bool = Field(True, description="是否已向患者告知")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")


class ConsentSignIn(BaseModel):
    """知情同意签署（P06）。"""

    signer_name: str = Field(..., min_length=2, max_length=64, description="签署人姓名")
    signer_relation: Literal[
        "SELF", "FATHER", "MOTHER", "GUARDIAN", "OTHER"
    ] = Field("SELF", description="签署人与患者关系")
    signer_id_card: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="签署人身份证号")
    signed_at: datetime = Field(..., description="签署时间")
    witness_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="见证人姓名")
    inform_doctor_id: int = Field(..., gt=0, description="告知医师 ID")
    inform_duration_minutes: Optional[int] = Field(
        None, ge=1, le=240, description="告知时长（分钟）")
    qa_record: Optional[List[Dict[str, str]]] = Field(
        None, description="患者提问与答复记录数组")
    signature_image_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="电子签名图片 ID")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_signer(self) -> "ConsentSignIn":
        """非本人签署必须有见证人。"""
        if self.signer_relation != "SELF" and not self.witness_name:
            raise ValueError("非本人签署必须填写见证人姓名")
        return self


class PlanQueryIn(BaseModel):
    """方案分页查询条件。"""

    keyword: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="姓名/方案号/手机号")
    refractive_type: Optional[RefractiveType] = Field(None, description="屈光术式码")
    status: Optional[PlanStatus] = Field(None, description="方案状态")
    chief_doctor_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="主刀医师 ID")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="科室 ID")
    patient_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="患者 ID")
    risk_level: Optional[RiskLevel] = Field(None, description="风险等级")
    start_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="创建日期起")
    end_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="创建日期止")
    pending_audit_only: bool = Field(False, description="仅看待审核")
    rsb_violated_only: bool = Field(False, description="仅看 RSB 红线违规")
    page: int = Field(1, ge=1, description="页码")
    page_size: int = Field(20, ge=1, le=200, description="每页条数")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_date_range(self) -> "PlanQueryIn":
        """日期区间起止有序。"""
        if self.start_date and self.end_date and self.start_date > self.end_date:
            raise ValueError("创建日期起不得晚于止")
        return self

class PlanBriefOut(BaseModel):
    """方案简要信息（列表用）。"""

    plan_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="方案号")

    operation_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="手术项目名称")
    eye_side: str = Field(..., description="手术眼别")
    status: str = Field(..., description="方案状态")
    chief_doctor_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="主刀医师姓名")
    min_rsb_um: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="双眼最小剩余基质床厚度（μm）")
    rsb_redline_violated: bool = Field(False, description="是否触发 RSB 红线")
    risk_level: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="风险等级")

    audited_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="审核通过时间")
    expire_at: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="方案有效期")

    os_wtw: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="左眼角膜直径（mm）")

class AblationOut(BaseModel):
    """角膜切削参数出参。"""

    pre_cylinder: Decimal = Field(..., description="术前柱镜（D）")

    tz_mm: Decimal = Field(..., description="过渡区外径 TZ（mm）")
    ablation_max_um: Decimal = Field(..., description="最大切削深度（μm）")
    ablation_total_um: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="组织切削总量（μm·mm²）")
    flap_thickness_um: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="瓣厚度（μm）")
    cap_thickness_um: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="帽厚度（μm）")
    rsb_um: Decimal = Field(..., description="剩余基质床厚度（μm）")
    rsb_status: str = Field(..., description="RSB 判定 PASS/WARN/VIOLATION/PENDING")
    thickness_margin_um: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="角膜厚度余量（μm）")
    feasible: bool = Field(..., description="是否可执行")
    infeasible_reasons: Optional[List[Any]] = Field(None, description="不可行原因数组")
    warn_flags: Optional[List[Any]] = Field(None, description="警告标记数组")
```

## 附录 A：表清单速查

| 编号 | 表名 | 中文名 | 主键 | 主干关系 | 写入方 | 只读方 | 预估量级 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4.2 | `m11_plan` | 手术方案主表 | `id` | 一患一次方案一单 | M11-04 | M06、M09、M16-04、M07、M11-05 | 8,000/年 |
| 4.3 | `m11_plan_preop_snap` | 方案术前基线快照表 | `id` | `plan_id` 1:1，不可变 | M11-04 | M11-04、M09 | 8,000/年 |
| 4.4 | `m11_plan_ablation` | 角膜切削参数表 | `id` | `plan_id` × `eye_side` 1:1 | M11-04 | M09、M06、M11-05、M11-07 | 15,000/年 |
| 4.5 | `m11_plan_ablation_calc` | 切削计算过程留痕表 | `id` | `plan_id` 1:N | M11-04 | M11-04、M11-07 | 40,000/年 |
| 4.6 | `m11_iol_calc` | 晶体度数计算表 | `id` | `patient_id` 当前唯一 | M11-04 | **M09-05（P-01）**、M06、M11-05 | 2,500/年 |
| 4.7 | `m11_iol_calc_detail` | 晶体计算结果明细表 | `id` | `iol_calc_id` 1:N | M11-04 | M11-04、M11-07 | 7,500/年 |
| 4.8 | `m11_plan_audit` | 方案审核表 | `id` | `plan_id` 1:N | M11-04 | 质控科、医政科、M20 | 20,000/年 |
| 4.9 | `m11_plan_version` | 方案版本表 | `id` | `plan_id` 1:N，不可变 | M11-04 | M11-04（P07）、M06、M09 | 24,000/年 |
| 4.10 | `m11_plan_risk` | 方案风险提示表 | `id` | `plan_id` 1:N | M11-04 | M11-04（P08）、审核、M11-05 | 60,000/年 |
| 4.11 | `m11_plan_consent_snap` | 知情同意方案快照表 | `id` | `plan_id` 1:1，固化不可变 | M11-04、M11-05 | 医政科、法务 | 8,000/年 |
| 4.12 | `m11_plan_log` | 方案操作日志表 | `id`+`created_at` | 审计旁路 | M11-04 全部 Service | M02 审计、质控科 | 200,000/年 |

**表名前缀核对：** 10 张 `m11_plan_*` + 1 张 `m11_iol_*`，与共享规范 §3.2 / §12 一致。

**跨子模块只读引用（不建表、不建外键）：**

| 被引用表 | 归属 | 引用字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mpi_patient` | M03-01 | `patient_id` | 患者主索引 |
| `mdm_surgery_operation` | M01-07 | `operation_code` | 屈光术式配置与分级 |
| `mdm_surgery_auth` | M01-07 | `chief_doctor_id` + `operation_code` | 医师手术授权 |
| `m11_preop_exam` | M11-02 | `preop_exam_id`、`preop_exam_no` | 术前检查与准入判定 |
| `m11_refractive_record` | M11-03 | `record_no` | 屈光档案 |
| `m10_optometry` | M10-01 | 经 M11-03 间接引用 | 验光单 |
| `mdm_material` | M01-04 | `iol_material_code` | 晶体型号与耗材 |
| M16 计费引擎 | M16 | — | 仅存 `_snap` 快照 |

## 附录 B：状态枚举汇总

| 枚举 | 字段 | 取值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 屈光术式 | `m11_plan.refractive_type` | `SMILE`/`FS_LASIK`/`TRANSPRK`/`SMART`/`ICL`/`PRL`/`CXL`/`PIOL`/`RK`/`OTHER_REFRACTIVE` | 见 B.1 |
| 方案状态 | `m11_plan.status` | `DRAFT`/`PENDING_AUDIT`/`AUDITED`/`REJECTED`/`EXPIRED`/`CANCELLED`/`SUPERSEDED` | 见 B.2 |
| 方案来源 | `m11_plan.plan_source` | `NEW`/`RE_TREATMENT`/`ENHANCEMENT`/`OTHERS_HOSPITAL` | 首次/再手术/增效/外院转来 |
| 眼别 | `eye_side` | `OD`/`OS`/`OU`/`NA` | 右眼/左眼/双眼/不适用 |
| 手术分级 | `m11_plan.operation_level` | `LEVEL_1`/`LEVEL_2`/`LEVEL_3`/`LEVEL_4` | 一至四级 |
| 风险等级 | `risk_level` | `LOW`/`MEDIUM`/`HIGH`/`FORBIDDEN` | 低/中/高/禁止 |
| 风险编码 | `m11_plan_risk.risk_code` | 20 项 | 见附录 D.2 |
| 风险类别 | `m11_plan_risk.risk_category` | `ABLATION_SAFETY`/`CORNEAL`/`OCULAR_SURFACE`/`OPTICAL_QUALITY`/`INTRAOCULAR`/`CONTRAINDICATION`/`PROCEDURAL` | 切削安全/角膜/眼表/视觉质量/眼内/禁忌/术中 |
| RSB 判定 | `m11_plan_ablation.rsb_status` | `PASS`/`WARN`/`VIOLATION`/`PENDING` | ≥300 / 250~300 / <250 / 未判定 |
| 切削算法 | `algo_code` | `MUNNERLYN`/`MROCHEN`/`TRANS_ASTIG`/`TOPO_GUIDED`/`WAVEFRONT` | 见 B.3 |
| 切削模式 | `optical_zone_type` | `STANDARD`/`ASPIRATION`/`TOPO_GUIDED`/`WAVEFRONT` | 标准/非球面/地形图引导/波前引导 |
| OZ 选择方式 | `oz_selected_by` | `AUTO`/`MANUAL` | 自动/医师指定 |
| 晶体公式 | `m11_iol_calc.formula_code` | `SRK_T`/`HOLLADAY_1`/`HOLLADAY_2`/`HAIGIS`/`BARRETT_UNIVERSAL_II`/`VENDOR_NOMOGRAM` | 见 B.4 |
| 散光目标 | `astigmatism_target` | `TORIC_NONE`/`TORIC_FULL`/`TORIC_PARTIAL` | 不矫正/全矫/部分矫正 |
| 偏差判定 | `m11_iol_calc.deviation_status` | `PENDING`/`WITHIN_TOLERANCE`/`EXCEED`/`JUSTIFIED` | 未判/≤0.25D/>0.25D/超差已说明 |
| 晶体计算状态 | `m11_iol_calc.status` | `DRAFT`/`CONFIRMED`/`SUPERSEDED`/`CANCELLED` | 草稿/已确认/已替代/已取消 |
| 审核动作 | `m11_plan_audit.audit_action` | `SUBMIT`/`AUDIT`/`APPROVE`/`REJECT`/`WITHDRAW` | 提交/审核/终审/驳回/撤回 |
| 审核结果 | `m11_plan_audit.audit_result` | `PENDING`/`PASSED`/`REJECTED` | 待审/通过/驳回 |
| 审核角色 | `m11_plan_audit.auditor_role` | `CHIEF_DOCTOR`/`SENIOR_DOCTOR`/`QC`/`MEDICAL_AFFAIRS` | 科主任/上级医师/质控/医政 |
| 同意书类型 | `consent_type` | `SURGERY`/`ANESTHESIA`/`IOL_IMPLANT`/`RE_TREATMENT` | 手术/麻醉/晶体植入/再手术 |
| 日志结果 | `m11_plan_log.result` | `SUCCESS`/`FAIL` | 成功/失败 |
| 操作人角色 | `m11_plan_log.operator_role` | `DOCTOR`/`CHIEF_DOCTOR`/`QC`/`NURSE`/`SYSTEM` | 医师/科主任/质控/护士/系统 |

### B.1 屈光术式码（M01-07 3.1.4，一字不差）

| 术式码 | 中文 | 类别 | 是否眼内 | 切削计算 | 晶体计算 | 随访基线 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SMILE` | 飞秒激光小切口基质透镜取出术 | 角膜激光 | 否 | 是（帽厚 + 切削） | 否 | 常规 |
| `FS_LASIK` | 飞秒制瓣 LASIK | 角膜激光 | 否 | 是（瓣厚 + 切削） | 否 | 常规 |
| `TRANSPRK` | 经上皮准分子激光角膜切削术 | 角膜激光 | 否 | 是（无瓣无帽） | 否 | 加强（Haze） |
| `SMART` | 智能脉冲技术表层手术 | 角膜激光 | 否 | 是（无瓣无帽） | 否 | 加强（Haze） |
| `ICL` | 有晶体眼后房型人工晶体植入术 | 眼内镜 | **是** | 否 | **是（厂商 nomogram）** | 含拱高监测 |
| `PRL` | 后房型屈光晶体植入术 | 眼内镜 | **是** | 否 | **是（厂商 nomogram）** | 含拱高监测 |
| `CXL` | 角膜交联术 | 角膜其他 | 否 | 否 | 否 | 含角膜形态 |
| `PIOL` | 屈光性人工晶体置换/植入 | 眼内镜 | **是** | 否 | **是（眼内公式）** | 常规 |
| `RK` | 放射状角膜切开术 | 角膜其他 | 否 | 否 | 否 | 常规 |
| `OTHER_REFRACTIVE` | 其他屈光手术 | 其他 | 否 | 否 | 否 | 常规 |

> **本子模块计算范围：** 仅 `SMILE`/`FS_LASIK`/`TRANSPRK`/`SMART` 走角膜切削计算（`m11_plan_ablation`）；仅 `ICL`/`PRL`/`PIOL` 走晶体度数计算（`m11_iol_calc`）。`CXL`/`RK`/`OTHER_REFRACTIVE` 只做方案记录、审核与风险提示，不做切削或晶体计算。

### B.2 方案状态机（7 态）

| 状态 | 中文 | 进入条件 | 允许的下一状态 | 可编辑 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `DRAFT` | 草稿 | 新建方案 | `PENDING_AUDIT`、`CANCELLED` | 是 |
| `PENDING_AUDIT` | 待审核 | 提交审核（生成版本快照） | `AUDITED`、`REJECTED`、`CANCELLED` | 否 |
| `AUDITED` | 已审核 | 审核通过且授权校验通过 | `EXPIRED`、`CANCELLED`、`SUPERSEDED` | 否 |
| `REJECTED` | 已驳回 | 审核驳回 | `PENDING_AUDIT`（修订后重提）、`CANCELLED` | 是（修订） |
| `EXPIRED` | 已过期 | 超过 `expire_at` | 终态 | 否 |
| `CANCELLED` | 已取消 | 患者放弃/改方案 | 终态 | 否 |
| `SUPERSEDED` | 已被替代 | 生成更新的有效方案 | 终态 | 否 |

### B.3 切削计算算法

| 算法码 | 中文 | 适用条件 | 特点 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MUNNERLYN` | Munnerlyn 公式 | 常规近视/散光，球柱镜 ≤ 6.00D | 经典抛物面模型，简单快速，高度数低估 |
| `MROCHEN` | Mrochen 修正模型 | 高度近视 > 6.00D | 引入角膜生物力学补偿，深度更准 |
| `TRANS_ASTIG` | 交叉柱镜法 | 散光 ≥ 1.50D | 分两次切削，减少组织去除不均 |
| `TOPO_GUIDED` | 地形图引导 | 角膜不规则、既往手术后再手术 | 按角膜高度图个性化切削 |
| `WAVEFRONT` | 波前引导 | 高阶像差明显者 | 按波前像差优化视觉质量 |

### B.4 晶体度数计算公式

| 公式码 | 中文 | 核心输入 | 适用条件 | 选择理由要点 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SRK_T` | SRK/T | AL、K、A 常数 | AL 22.0~26.0mm | 眼轴正常范围误差最小（约 0.4D） |
| `HOLLADAY_1` | Holladay 1 | AL、K、SF | AL 22.0~26.0mm | 引入医师因子 SF 校正 |
| `HOLLADAY_2` | Holladay 2 | AL、K、ACD、LT、WTW、年龄、术前屈光 | AL < 22 或 > 26mm | 7 变量，极端眼轴首选 |
| `HAIGIS` | Haigis | AL、ACD、K | 需 ACD 测量 | 对 ACD 敏感，前房异常首选 |
| `BARRETT_UNIVERSAL_II` | Barrett Universal II | AL、K、ACD、LT、WTW | 全范围 | 多变量回归，近年推荐度最高 |
| `VENDOR_NOMOGRAM` | 厂商定制 nomogram | 各厂商专有 | ICL/PRL | 厂商按晶体型号提供的专用算法 |

### B.5 方案审核检查表（11 项）

| # | 检查项 | 判定标准 | 不通过影响 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 术式与屈光度匹配 | 术式适用范围内 | 驳回 |
| 2 | 术前准入结论 | `admission_result = 'PASS'` | 驳回 |
| 3 | 禁忌症复查 | 无圆锥可疑、无活动性炎症 | 驳回 |
| 4 | RSB 达标 | `rsb_um >= 250μm` 且裕度充足 | 驳回 |
| 5 | 光学区合理性 | OZ 与暗瞳匹配 | 驳回 |
| 6 | 角膜厚度余量 | `thickness_margin_um > 0` | 驳回 |
| 7 | ICL 前房深度 | `ACD >= 2.80mm`（眼内手术） | 驳回 |
| 8 | 晶体度数偏差 | `deviation_status` 非 `EXCEED` | 驳回 |
| 9 | 医师授权有效 | M01-07 `auth/check` 通过 | 驳回 |
| 10 | 风险已告知 | 阻断级风险 `patient_notified = TRUE` | 驳回 |
| 11 | 知情同意已签 | `is_sealed = TRUE` | 不得终审通过 |

### B.6 日志动作（28 个）

| 分组 | 动作码 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 方案主档 | `CREATE_PLAN` | 新建方案 |
| 方案主档 | `UPDATE_PLAN` | 修改方案基础信息 |
| 方案主档 | `SNAPSHOT_PREOP` | 生成术前基线快照 |
| 方案主档 | `CANCEL_PLAN` | 取消方案 |
| 方案主档 | `EXPIRE_PLAN` | 方案过期 |
| 方案主档 | `SUPERSEDE_PLAN` | 方案被替代 |
| 切削计算 | `CALC_ABLATION` | 执行切削计算 |
| 切削计算 | `RECALC_ABLATION` | 重新计算切削参数 |
| 切削计算 | `ADOPT_ABLATION` | 采纳切削计算结果 |
| 切削计算 | `CONFIRM_ABLATION` | 确认切削参数 |
| 晶体计算 | `CALC_IOL` | 执行晶体度数计算 |
| 晶体计算 | `RECALC_IOL` | 重新计算晶体度数 |
| 晶体计算 | `SELECT_IOL_POWER` | 选择实际晶体度数 |
| 晶体计算 | `CONFIRM_IOL` | 确认晶体计算 |
| 晶体计算 | `QUERY_IOL_MATERIAL` | 查询晶体型号库存 |
| 审核 | `SUBMIT_AUDIT` | 提交审核 |
| 审核 | `AUDIT_PASS` | 审核通过 |
| 审核 | `AUDIT_REJECT` | 审核驳回 |
| 审核 | `APPROVE_PLAN` | 终审通过 |
| 审核 | `WITHDRAW_AUDIT` | 撤回审核 |
| 审核 | `AUTH_RECHECK` | 重新做医师授权校验 |
| 版本 | `CREATE_VERSION` | 生成版本快照 |
| 版本 | `COMPARE_VERSION` | 版本对比 |
| 风险 | `GENERATE_RISK` | 生成风险提示 |
| 风险 | `ACK_RISK` | 医师确认风险 |
| 风险 | `NOTIFY_PATIENT` | 告知患者风险 |
| 同意 | `SIGN_CONSENT` | 知情同意签署 |
| 同意 | `REVOKE_CONSENT` | 撤销知情同意 |

## 附录 C：关键索引与查询

### C.1 查询一：患者当前有效方案（P-05 核心）

```sql
-- 入参：$patient_id
SELECT id, plan_no, refractive_type, eye_side, status,
       current_version, min_rsb_um, risk_level,
       audited_at, expire_at
FROM m11_plan
WHERE patient_id = $patient_id
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
  AND status IN ('DRAFT', 'PENDING_AUDIT', 'AUDITED')
ORDER BY
    CASE status WHEN 'AUDITED' THEN 1 WHEN 'PENDING_AUDIT' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,
    created_at DESC
LIMIT 1;
```

**走索引：** `idx_m11_plan_patient_active (patient_id)` 部分索引。

### C.2 查询二：晶体计算（P-01 核心）

```sql
-- 入参：$patient_id，返回当前有效计算的理论度数
SELECT calc_no, patient_id, refractive_type, eye_side,
       formula_code, axial_length_mm, anterior_chamber_depth_mm,
       white_to_white_mm, theoretical_power_d, selected_power_d,
       deviation_d, deviation_status, deviation_tolerance_d,
       iol_model, status, confirmed_at
FROM m11_iol_calc
WHERE patient_id = $patient_id
  AND is_current = TRUE
  AND is_deleted = FALSE;
```

**走索引：** `uk_m11_iol_calc_current (patient_id)` 唯一索引，退化为单行查找，P95 ≤ 200ms 有保障。

### C.3 查询三：待审核方案队列

```sql
-- 入参：$auditor_level、$dept_id
SELECT p.id, p.plan_no, p.patient_name, p.refractive_type, p.eye_side,
       p.risk_level, p.min_rsb_um, p.submitted_at, p.current_version,
       a.id AS audit_id, a.audit_deadline
FROM m11_plan p
JOIN m11_plan_audit a
  ON a.plan_id = p.id AND a.audit_result = 'PENDING' AND a.is_deleted = FALSE
WHERE p.status = 'PENDING_AUDIT'
  AND p.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND a.audit_level = $auditor_level
  AND ($dept_id IS NULL OR p.dept_id = $dept_id)
ORDER BY p.submitted_at ASC
LIMIT 100;
```

**走索引：** `idx_m11_plan_audit_pending (audit_level, submitted_at)`。

### C.4 查询四：RSB 边缘案例抽查（质控）

```sql
-- RSB 在 250~300μm 之间的高风险方案（质控必查）
SELECT p.plan_no, p.patient_name, p.refractive_type,
       ab.eye_side, ab.oz_mm, ab.ablation_max_um,
       ab.rsb_um, ab.rsb_status, ab.thickness_margin_um,
       p.chief_doctor_name
FROM m11_plan_ablation ab
JOIN m11_plan p ON p.id = ab.plan_id
WHERE ab.rsb_status = 'WARN'
  AND ab.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND p.is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY ab.rsb_um ASC;
```

**走索引：** `idx_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_warn (rsb_um)` 部分索引。

### C.5 查询五：晶体度数偏差超阈值清单（M09 核对异常追溯）

```sql
SELECT ic.calc_no, ic.patient_name, ic.refractive_type, ic.eye_side,
       ic.theoretical_power_d, ic.selected_power_d,
       ic.deviation_d, ic.deviation_status, ic.deviation_reason,
       ic.formula_code, ic.confirmed_at
FROM m11_iol_calc ic
WHERE ic.deviation_status IN ('EXCEED', 'JUSTIFIED')
  AND ic.is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY ic.calc_at DESC;
```

**走索引：** `idx_m11_iol_calc_deviation (deviation_status, calc_at DESC)`。

### C.6 查询六：方案版本对比

```sql
-- 入参：$plan_id, $from_version, $to_version
SELECT v.version_no, v.refractive_type,
       v.od_oz_mm, v.os_oz_mm,
       v.od_ablation_max_um, v.os_ablation_max_um,
       v.od_rsb_um, v.os_rsb_um,
       v.risk_level, v.change_reason, v.created_at,
       v.diff_field_count
FROM m11_plan_version v
WHERE v.plan_id = $plan_id
  AND v.version_no BETWEEN $from_version AND $to_version
  AND v.is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY v.version_no ASC;
```

**走索引：** `uk_m11_plan_version_no (plan_id, version_no)`。

### C.7 查询七：阻断级风险未确认清单

```sql
SELECT r.plan_id, p.plan_no, p.patient_name,
       r.risk_code, r.risk_name, r.risk_level,
       r.trigger_value, r.trigger_unit, r.patient_notified
FROM m11_plan_risk r
JOIN m11_plan p ON p.id = r.plan_id
WHERE r.is_blocking = TRUE
  AND r.acknowledged = FALSE
  AND r.is_active = TRUE
  AND r.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND p.is_deleted = FALSE
ORDER BY p.submitted_at ASC;
```

**走索引：** `idx_m11_plan_risk_unack (plan_id) WHERE is_blocking AND NOT acknowledged`。

### C.8 查询八：术式 × 风险分布统计

```sql
SELECT p.refractive_type,
       r.risk_code,
       count(*) AS hit_count,
       count(*) FILTER (WHERE r.risk_level = 'FORBIDDEN') AS forbidden_count
FROM m11_plan p
JOIN m11_plan_risk r ON r.plan_id = p.id
WHERE p.created_at >= $start_time
  AND p.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND r.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY p.refractive_type, r.risk_code
ORDER BY p.refractive_type, hit_count DESC;
```

**走索引：** `idx_m11_plan_risk_code (risk_code, created_at DESC)`。

### C.9 索引命中与容量估算

| 查询场景 | 主要索引 | 扫描行数估算 | 目标 P95 |
|---|---|---|---|
| P-01 晶体计算 | `uk_m11_iol_calc_current` | 1 | ≤100ms |
| P-04 方案详情 | `uk_m11_plan_no` + 从表索引 | ≤30 | ≤150ms |
| P-05 患者最新方案 | `idx_m11_plan_patient_active` | ≤10 | ≤120ms |
| 待审核队列 | `idx_m11_plan_audit_pending` | ≤100 | ≤120ms |
| RSB 边缘抽查 | `idx_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_warn` | ≤500 | ≤150ms |
| 偏差超阈值清单 | `idx_m11_iol_calc_deviation` | ≤200 | ≤150ms |
| 版本对比 | `uk_m11_plan_version_no` | ≤20 | ≤80ms |
| 风险未确认 | `idx_m11_plan_risk_unack` | ≤50 | ≤100ms |
| 术式风险分布 | `idx_m11_plan_risk_code` | 5,000~20,000 | ≤800ms |

## 附录 D：配置参数与字典

### D.1 系统参数

| 参数键 | 中文 | 默认值 | 说明 | 可调整 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `plan.valid.days` | 方案有效期（天） | `90` | 审核通过后 N 天内有效 | 是 |
| `plan.audit.levels.low` | 低风险方案审核级数 | `2` | 二级审核 | 否 |
| `plan.audit.levels.high` | 高风险方案审核级数 | `3` | 三级审核（含科主任终审） | 否 |
| `plan.audit.sla.hours` | 单级审核时限（小时） | `24` | 超时标 `is_overdue` | 是 |
| `plan.rsb.redline.um` | RSB 红线阈值（μm） | `250` | **不可调低** | 否 |
| `plan.rsb.warn.um` | RSB 告警阈值（μm） | `300` | 低于则标 WARN | 是（≥250） |
| `plan.oz.min.mm` | 光学区下限（mm） | `5.50` | 设备下限 | 否 |
| `plan.oz.max.mm` | 光学区上限（mm） | `8.00` | 设备上限 | 否 |
| `plan.tz.max.mm` | 过渡区外径上限（mm） | `9.50` | 广域切削 | 否 |
| `plan.tz.min.width.mm` | 最小过渡区宽度（单侧，mm） | `0.80` | 平滑过渡最低要求 | 否 |
| `plan.pupil.oz.gap.mm` | OZ 与暗瞳最小差值（mm） | `0.50` | OZ 建议 ≥ 暗瞳 + 0.5 | 是 |
| `plan.ablation.max.um` | 最大切削深度上限（μm） | `200` | 超过直接不可行 | 否 |
| `iol.deviation.tolerance.d` | 晶体度数偏差容忍（D） | `0.25` | M09 核对判据 | 是（≤1.00） |
| `iol.acd.min.mm` | ICL 前房深度下限（mm） | `2.80` | 低于则禁忌 | 否 |
| `iol.wtw.min.mm` | ICL 角膜直径下限（mm） | `10.00` | 低于则尺寸不足 | 否 |
| `iol.wtw.max.mm` | ICL 角膜直径上限（mm） | `13.50` | 超过则尺寸超限 | 否 |
| `iol.endothelial.min` | 内皮细胞计数下限（个/mm²） | `2000` | 低于则禁忌 | 否 |
| `iol.formula.min.count` | 最少交叉验证公式数 | `2` | 少于则提示 | 是 |
| `iol.outlier.delta.d` | 公式离群判定差值（D） | `0.75` | 与主用公式差超此值标离群 | 是 |
| `risk.pupil.large.mm` | 大瞳孔阈值（mm） | `6.50` | 暗瞳超过则提示 | 是 |
| `risk.hoa.medium.um` | 高阶像差中风险阈值（μm） | `0.30` | RMS 超过则提示 | 是 |
| `risk.tbtt.dry.sec` | 干眼 TBUT 阈值（秒） | `5.0` | 低于则干眼风险 | 是 |
| `consent.min.inform.minutes` | 最短告知时长（分钟） | `5` | 低于则提示未充分告知 | 是 |
| `log.retention.months` | 日志保留月数 | `24` | 分区归档 | 是 |

### D.2 风险编码字典（20 项）

| 编码 | 中文 | 类别 | 等级 | 阻断 | 触发条件 | 阈值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RSB_BELOW_REDLINE` | 剩余基质床低于红线 | `ABLATION_SAFETY` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | `rsb_um < 250` | 250μm |
| `RSB_MARGIN_LOW` | 剩余基质床裕度偏低 | `ABLATION_SAFETY` | `HIGH` | 否 | `rsb_um < 300` | 300μm |
| `THICKNESS_CORNEAL_THIN` | 角膜厚度不足 | `CORNEAL` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | `cct_um < 480` | 480μm |
| `ABLATION_DEPTH_HIGH` | 切削深度过大 | `ABLATION_SAFETY` | `HIGH` | 否 | `ablation_max_um > 120` | 120μm |
| `OZ_SMALLER_THAN_PUPIL` | 光学区小于暗瞳 | `OPTICAL_QUALITY` | `MEDIUM` | 否 | `oz_mm < pupil_scotopic` | — |
| `PUPIL_LARGE` | 瞳孔过大 | `OPTICAL_QUALITY` | `MEDIUM` | 否 | `pupil_scotopic > 6.50` | 6.50mm |
| `HOA_HIGH` | 高阶像差明显 | `OPTICAL_QUALITY` | `MEDIUM` | 否 | `hoa_rms > 0.30` | 0.30μm |
| `DRY_EYE` | 干眼 | `OCULAR_SURFACE` | `MEDIUM` | 否 | `tbtt < 5.0` 或 `has_dry_eye` | 5.0s |
| `KERATOCONUS_SUSPECT` | 圆锥角膜可疑 | `CONTRAINDICATION` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | `keratoconus_suspect = TRUE` | — |
| `CORNEAL_IRREGULAR` | 角膜形态不规则 | `CONTRAINDICATION` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | 地形图不规则 | — |
| `ACD_SHALLOW` | 前房深度不足 | `INTRAOCULAR` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | `acd < 2.80`（眼内手术） | 2.80mm |
| `WTW_OUT_OF_RANGE` | 角膜直径超范围 | `INTRAOCULAR` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | `wtw < 10.00` 或 `> 13.50` | 10.00~13.50mm |
| `ENDOTHELIAL_LOW` | 内皮细胞计数偏低 | `INTRAOCULAR` | `HIGH` | 否 | `endothelial < 2000` | 2000 个/mm² |
| `HIGH_MYOPIA` | 超高度近视 | `ABLATION_SAFETY` | `HIGH` | 否 | `abs(sphere) > 8.00` | 8.00D |
| `HIGH_ASTIGMATISM` | 高散光 | `ABLATION_SAFETY` | `MEDIUM` | 否 | `abs(cylinder) > 3.00` | 3.00D |
| `PREVIOUS_SURGERY` | 既往屈光手术史 | `CONTRAINDICATION` | `HIGH` | 否 | `previous_refractive_surgery` | — |
| `AUTO_IMMUNE` | 自身免疫病 | `CONTRAINDICATION` | `HIGH` | 否 | `has_autoimmune` | — |
| `PREGNANT` | 妊娠/哺乳期 | `CONTRAINDICATION` | `FORBIDDEN` | 是 | `pregnant = TRUE` | — |
| `CONTACT_LENS_STOP_SHORT` | 隐形眼镜停戴不足 | `PROCEDURAL` | `MEDIUM` | 否 | 软镜 <7 天或硬镜 <21 天 | 7/21 天 |
| `IOL_POWER_DEVIATION` | 晶体度数偏差超阈值 | `INTRAOCULAR` | `HIGH` | 否 | `deviation > 0.25` | 0.25D |

### D.3 字典项

| 字典类别 | 名称 | 取值 |
|---|---|---|
| `REFRACTIVE_TYPE` | 屈光术式 | 见附录 B.1（10 个） |
| `PLAN_STATUS` | 方案状态 | 见附录 B.2（7 个） |
| `ALGO_CODE` | 切削算法 | 见附录 B.3（5 个） |
| `IOL_FORMULA` | 晶体公式 | 见附录 B.4（6 个） |
| `RISK_CODE` | 风险编码 | 见附录 D.2（20 个） |
| `AUDIT_ACTION` | 审核动作 | `SUBMIT`/`AUDIT`/`APPROVE`/`REJECT`/`WITHDRAW` |
| `REJECT_ITEM` | 驳回问题项 | `OZ_INAPPROPRIATE`/`RSB_MARGIN_LOW`/`POWER_DEVIATION`/`AUTH_INVALID`/`CONSENT_MISSING`/`ACD_SHALLOW`/`THICKNESS_INSUFFICIENT`/`OTHER` |
| `IOL_SIZE` | ICL 尺寸规格 | `12.10`/`12.60`/`13.20`/`13.70`/`14.20`（mm） |
| `CONSENT_TYPE` | 同意书类型 | `SURGERY`/`ANESTHESIA`/`IOL_IMPLANT`/`RE_TREATMENT` |

---

## 附录 E：建表依赖顺序与 Alembic 迁移

### E.1 建表依赖顺序（4 层）

| 层 | 表 | 依赖的外部表（须先建） | 内部依赖 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **第 0 层（外部）** | — | `mpi_patient`（M03-01）、`mdm_surgery_operation`（M01-07）、`mdm_surgery_auth`（M01-07）、`m11_preop_exam`（M11-02）、`m11_refractive_record`（M11-03）、`mdm_material`（M01-04） | 只读引用，不建外键 |
| **第 1 层（无内部依赖）** | `m11_plan` | 第 0 层 | 方案主档 |
| **第 2 层（依赖方案主档）** | `m11_plan_preop_snap`、`m11_plan_ablation`、`m11_iol_calc`、`m11_plan_audit`、`m11_plan_version`、`m11_plan_risk`、`m11_plan_consent_snap`、`m11_plan_log` | 第 0 层 + `m11_plan` | 均挂 `plan_id` |
| **第 3 层（依赖第 2 层）** | `m11_plan_ablation_calc`、`m11_iol_calc_detail` | 上层 | 挂 `plan_id` / `iol_calc_id` |

**物理建表顺序：**

```text
① m11_plan
② m11_plan_preop_snap
③ m11_plan_ablation
④ m11_plan_ablation_calc
⑤ m11_iol_calc
⑥ m11_iol_calc_detail
⑦ m11_plan_audit
⑧ m11_plan_version
⑨ m11_plan_risk
⑩ m11_plan_consent_snap
⑪ m11_plan_log（分区表 + 4 个初始分区 + DEFAULT 分区）
```

### E.2 Alembic 迁移脚本

```python

down_revision = "20261101_m11_03"

        sa.Column("auth_check_passed", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,

        sa.Column("rsb_redline_violated", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,

        sa.Column("current_version", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="1"),

    op.create_check_constraint(

    # ③ m11_plan_ablation（★ RSB 红线约束）
    op.create_table(

        "OR (rsb_status = 'WARN' AND rsb_um >= 250.00 AND rsb_um < 300.00) "
        "OR (rsb_status = 'PASS' AND rsb_um >= 300.00) "
        "OR (rsb_status = 'PENDING')",
    )

    # ④ m11_plan_ablation_calc
    op.create_table(
        "m11_plan_ablation_calc",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),

        sa.Column("white_to_white_mm", sa.Numeric(6, 2)),

        sa.Column("deviation_d", sa.Numeric(6, 2)),

    # ★ P-01 主力索引：每患者仅一条当前有效计算

        "ck_m11_iol_calc_tolerance", "m11_iol_calc",
        "deviation_tolerance_d > 0 AND deviation_tolerance_d <= 1.00")

    # ⑥ m11_iol_calc_detail
    op.create_table(
        "m11_iol_calc_detail",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),

    op.create_index("uk_m11_iol_calc_detail_primary", "m11_iol_calc_detail",
                    ["iol_calc_id", "eye_side"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "is_primary = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE"))

    # ⑦ m11_plan_audit
    op.create_table(
        "m11_plan_audit",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("plan_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("plan_version", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("audit_action", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("audit_level", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("required_levels", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="2"),
        sa.Column("audit_result", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="PENDING"),
        sa.Column("auditor_id", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("reject_reason", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("reject_items", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("checklist_result", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("auth_rechecked", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("audit_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.func.now()),
        sa.Column("is_overdue", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
    )
    op.create_index("idx_m11_plan_audit_pending", "m11_plan_audit",
                    ["audit_level", "submitted_at"],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "audit_result = 'PENDING' AND is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_check_constraint(
        "ck_m11_plan_audit_reject", "m11_plan_audit",
        "audit_result <> 'REJECTED' OR (reject_reason IS NOT NULL "
        "AND char_length(btrim(reject_reason)) >= 10 "
        "AND reject_items IS NOT NULL AND jsonb_array_length(reject_items) > 0)")

    # ⑧ m11_plan_version
    op.create_table(
        "m11_plan_version",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("plan_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("version_no", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("refractive_type", sa.String(24), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("eye_side", sa.String(8), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("snapshot_json", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("snapshot_hash", sa.String(64)),
        sa.Column("previous_version_no", sa.Integer()),
        sa.Column("diff_summary", postgresql.JSONB()),
        sa.Column("change_reason", sa.String(512)),
        sa.Column("od_rsb_um", sa.Numeric(6, 2)),
        sa.Column("os_rsb_um", sa.Numeric(6, 2)),
        sa.Column("is_current", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("is_audited", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.func.now()),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_plan_version_no", "m11_plan_version",
                    ["plan_id", "version_no"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_index("uk_m11_plan_version_current", "m11_plan_version",
                    ["plan_id"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "is_current = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE"))

    # ⑨ m11_plan_risk
    op.create_table(
        "m11_plan_risk",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("plan_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("eye_side", sa.String(8), nullable=False, server_default="NA"),
        sa.Column("risk_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("risk_name", sa.String(128), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("risk_category", sa.String(24), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("risk_level", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("is_blocking", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("trigger_value", sa.Numeric(10, 2)),
        sa.Column("threshold_value", sa.Numeric(10, 2)),
        sa.Column("acknowledged", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("patient_notified", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("is_active", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.true()),
        sa.Column("resolved", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.func.now()),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_plan_risk_item", "m11_plan_risk",
                    ["plan_id", "eye_side", "risk_code"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "is_active = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_index("idx_m11_plan_risk_unack", "m11_plan_risk", ["plan_id"],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text(
                        "is_blocking = TRUE AND acknowledged = FALSE"))
    # ★ FORBIDDEN 级必须阻断
    op.create_check_constraint(
        "ck_m11_plan_risk_blocking_ck", "m11_plan_risk",
        "risk_level <> 'FORBIDDEN' OR is_blocking = TRUE")

    # ⑩ m11_plan_consent_snap
    op.create_table(
        "m11_plan_consent_snap",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("plan_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("patient_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("snapshot_json", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("snapshot_hash", sa.String(64)),
        sa.Column("risk_codes_snapshot", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("signer_name", sa.String(64), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("signed_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("inform_doctor_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("pdf_file_id", sa.BigInteger()),
        sa.Column("is_sealed", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("is_revoked", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.false()),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m11_plan_consent_snap_plan", "m11_plan_consent_snap",
                    ["plan_id"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))
    # ★ 已固化记录不可逻辑删除（证据保全）
    op.create_check_constraint(
        "ck_m11_plan_consent_immutable", "m11_plan_consent_snap",
        "is_sealed = FALSE OR is_deleted = FALSE")

    # ⑪ m11_plan_log（分区表 + DEFAULT 兜底）
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log (
            id             BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            log_no         VARCHAR(48),
            plan_id        BIGINT,
            plan_no        VARCHAR(32),
            patient_id     BIGINT,
            entity_type    VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            entity_id      BIGINT,
            action         VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
            action_desc    VARCHAR(256),
            changed_fields JSONB,
            snapshot       JSONB,
            operator_id    BIGINT,
            operator_name  VARCHAR(64),
            trace_id       VARCHAR(64),
            result         VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SUCCESS',
            error_code     VARCHAR(16),
            error_message  VARCHAR(512),
            duration_ms    INT,
            created_at     TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at)
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202611 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
            FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01')
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202612 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
            FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-12-01') TO ('2027-01-01')
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202701 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
            FOR VALUES FROM ('2027-01-01') TO ('2027-02-01')
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_p202702 PARTITION OF m11_plan_log
            FOR VALUES FROM ('2027-02-01') TO ('2027-03-01')
    """)
    # ★ DEFAULT 分区兜底（共享规范硬要求）
    op.execute("CREATE TABLE m11_plan_log_pdefault PARTITION OF m11_plan_log DEFAULT")
    op.execute("""
        CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_plan ON m11_plan_log (plan_id, created_at DESC)
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE INDEX idx_m11_plan_log_fail ON m11_plan_log (created_at)
        WHERE result = 'FAIL'
    """)


def downgrade() -> None:
    """按逆序回滚。"""
    op.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS m11_plan_log CASCADE")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_consent_snap")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_risk")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_version")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_audit")
    op.drop_table("m11_iol_calc_detail")
    op.drop_table("m11_iol_calc")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_ablation_calc")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_ablation")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan_preop_snap")
    op.drop_table("m11_plan")
```

### E.3 上线前检查清单（12 项）

| # | 检查项 | 验证方式 | 期望结果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 11 张表全部建成 | `SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_name LIKE 'm11_plan_%' OR table_name LIKE 'm11_iol_%'` | ≥ 11（不含日志分区） |
| 2 | **RSB 红线约束生效** | `INSERT` 一条 `rsb_um = 240` 的切削参数 | 违反 `ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_redline` |
| 3 | **RSB 公式闭环约束生效** | `UPDATE` 只改切削深度不改 RSB | 违反 `ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_formula` |
| 4 | **RSB 状态一致性** | `INSERT` `rsb_um = 240` 但 `rsb_status = 'PASS'` | 违反 `ck_m11_plan_ablation_rsb_status` |
| 5 | **P-01 唯一当前索引** | `INSERT` 同患者两条 `is_current = TRUE` | 违反 `uk_m11_iol_calc_current` |
| 6 | **晶体公式理由约束** | `INSERT` `formula_reason` 只有 5 字 | 违反 `ck_m11_iol_calc_reason` |
| 7 | **偏差一致性约束** | `INSERT` 偏差与选择减理论不符 | 违反 `ck_m11_iol_calc_deviation` |
| 8 | **FORBIDDEN 必阻断** | `INSERT` `risk_level = 'FORBIDDEN'` 但 `is_blocking = FALSE` | 违反 `ck_m11_plan_risk_blocking_ck` |
| 9 | **同意快照不可删** | `UPDATE` 已固化同意记录 `is_deleted = TRUE` | 违反 `ck_m11_plan_consent_immutable` |
| 10 | 日志 DEFAULT 分区存在 | `SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class WHERE relname LIKE 'm11_plan_log_p%'` | ≥ 5（4 个月 + default） |
| 11 | 中文模糊检索可用 | `SELECT * FROM m11_plan WHERE patient_name % '张'` | 正常返回 |
| 12 | 触发器维护 `updated_at` | `UPDATE` 任意行后查 `updated_at` | 已更新 |

## 附录 F：文档维护约定

| # | 约定 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **表结构变更以本文件为准** | 详细设计第 4 章仅指引块，任何字段变更必须同步本文件与《M11 数据结构设计汇总》 |
| 2 | 表数口径单一 | 本文件表数 = `### 4.x 表：` 标题数 = 表清单行数 = 11 |
| 3 | 新增表须先登记 | 新表须先在共享规范 §12 登记前缀与归属，再在本文件添加章节 |
| 4 | 禁物理外键与物理删除 | 跨模块与模块内均不建物理外键；删除一律 `is_deleted = TRUE` |
| 5 | 禁 MySQL 语法 | 一律 PostgreSQL 16；自增用 `GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY`，布尔用 `BOOLEAN`，时间用 `TIMESTAMP`，禁止任何 MySQL 专有建表子句 |
| 6 | 金额字段带 `_snap` 后缀 | M11 不计算金额，只存 M16 返回快照 |
| 7 | 不可变表不得提供 UPDATE 语义 | `m11_plan_preop_snap`、`m11_plan_version` 插入后只读；`m11_plan_consent_snap` 固化后冻结 |
| 8 | RSB 红线不可调低 | `plan.rsb.redline.um` 系统参数下限 250μm，数据库 CHECK 与系统参数双重锁定 |
| 9 | 分区表必须有 DEFAULT 分区 | `m11_plan_log` 必须始终存在 `m11_plan_log_pdefault`，缺失视为严重故障 |
| 10 | 单位约定 | 屈光度 `D` 写 `-4.25D`；轴位 `°` 写 `180°`；眼别 `OD/OS/OU`；厚度一律 `μm` 写 `520μm`；眼轴 `mm` |
| 11 | 枚举中文化 | 库内一律英文大写枚举值，中文对照维护在附录 B 与附录 D.2 |
| 12 | 变更须更新附录 E | 任何建表顺序或依赖变化必须同步附录 E.1 与 E.2 的迁移脚本 |
